# Delegation invariant

For every substantive task unit: decompose it, resolve authority and repo,
inspect live eligible workers/executors, dispatch to the cheapest adequate
lane, verify, and escalate only on recorded evidence.

Before starting bounded implementation, refactoring, debugging, test
authoring, review, repository reconnaissance, batch, scan, test, index,
evaluation, or simulation work, run:

```text
aoteru preflight "<task>" [--repo <repo-id>]
```

The equivalent backend call is `POST /api/estate/preflight`. Follow its live
recommendation. Codex implementation requires an existing active repo write
lease; preflight and execution must never acquire one implicitly. Dispatch it
with the equivalent `POST /api/estate/run` envelope or:

```text
aoteru ask "<task>" --repo <id> --capability code-strong --allow-paid --implementation
```

Retain work in the controller only for intent, architecture or methodological
judgement, ambiguity resolution, cross-worker synthesis, arbitration, or final
acceptance. Record a concrete `nondelegation_reason` whenever retaining an
otherwise eligible unit.

`docs/aoteru-model-host-routing-contract.md` is the routing and authority
contract. Do not duplicate or override it here.

Subscription model version/tier is never a task-controlled variable. Apply
`docs/subscription-model-currency-policy.md`: only effort is selected, use
the newest authenticated model and fail closed rather than fall back. Local
models retain their separate qualification policy.

**Usage-limit failover:** For a verified Codex quota/usage-limit response, do not
park otherwise eligible work until reset. Apply the existing routing contract's
"Codex usage-limit → GLM failover" section: prefer an actually qualified GLM
implementation/review lane with the same task scope and lease; if unavailable,
continue independent deterministic/other qualified work and preserve the exact
review debt. Never treat an unmerged GLM candidate, launcher smoke test,
alias name, or credential discovery as worker qualification.


## Temporary operating priority: ship the existing backlog (2026-10-17)

**Operator instruction; effective until the accepted backlog is shipped, or the operator explicitly changes this priority.** Shipping takes precedence over optional refinement, optimisation, refactoring, speculative research, workflow redesign, prompt tuning, new feature ideation and new planning artifacts. This changes *work selection*, not safety, authority or acceptance thresholds.

- **Scope and baseline:** apply to Odysseus-owned work in `automation/review/agent-tasks/{ready,inbox,blocked}` and executable workstreams in its canonical programme queues. At the 2026-10-17 baseline (`dev` at `e8ba6a2e08084f857b2739c99d8fa35318cea6b0`) there were 8 `ready`, 15 `inbox`, and 8 `blocked` agent-task cards. Reconcile with live state; some inbox cards may be duplicate, superseded or already delivered. Domain-specific queues remain owned by their domain repositories.
- **Select work to deliver:** finish already-started and `ready` items that have a viable end-to-end delivery path before starting an unrelated improvement. Triage `inbox` only enough to identify accepted, high-value shippable work, duplicates and genuine dependencies. Choose the next slice by *time to verified delivery*, user value and dependency unblocking, rather than elegance of the architecture. Prefer a small complete vertical result over an extensively polished partial one.
- **Meaning of shipped:** acceptance conditions satisfied; minimum relevant tests and safety/security checks pass; changes merged and deployed/real-path exercised when the task requires it; queue/programme status and evidence updated. A draft, plan, PR, untested implementation, or a status-only move to `done` is **not shipped**. Do not weaken existing tests, review/ratification conditions, write leases, release controls, research validity or evidence floors to gain throughput.
- **Blockers and new work:** record an exact external blocker once and immediately advance another eligible item. Keep blocked accepted work in the unfinished backlog. Capture newly proposed nonessential work in `inbox` but defer execution; admit new work ahead of existing items only to fix a safety/correctness failure, fulfil an acceptance gate, or unblock delivery. Do not repeatedly optimise the delivery machinery itself unless measurement shows it blocks shipping.
- **Progress and exit:** each dispatch/review should identify the concrete item and expected shipping evidence; report counts of shipped, accepted remaining, untriaged, and blocked without gaming classifications. Maintain shipping-first until **all accepted baseline work and essential delivery dependencies** are genuinely shipped or individually closed as duplicate/obsolete/rejected with recorded justification and required authority. An external blocker, a green test suite, or an empty `ready` directory alone does not terminate this policy. Revert to normal refinement priority only after a documented final backlog reconciliation or explicit operator instruction.

## Global initialising prompt register

Odysseus owns the cross-repository initialising-prompt register:

- registry: `config/initialising-prompts.yaml`
- contract: `docs/initialising-prompt-register.md`
- immutable prompt bodies: `docs/initialising-prompts/**`
- append-only application traces: `evals/prompt-applications/**`
- opportunistic prompt exemplars: `evals/prompt-exemplars/**`

At the start of a substantive session, check whether the target repository/task matches an active registered initialiser. If the operator supplied a registered initialiser, resolve it by `prompt_id` + `version`; do not silently substitute another version.

At substantive session closeout, record one application trace when a registered initialiser was actually used. The trace records repository/branch, start/end commits, outcome, stop reason, models/routes used, verification evidence, and a rubric-based agent rating. Operator rating is separate and optional; never invent it.

**Prompt evolution is a graph loop, not in-place mutation.** Every completed application must create an evolution event under `evals/prompt-evolution/**`. Failure tags, weak rating dimensions and explicit operator feedback become evidence for a child prompt version. Clean runs create a reinforcement edge instead of cosmetic wording churn. Existing prompt versions and application traces remain immutable.

A child may become the active head only when its delta is traceable to observed performance and it preserves repository scope, authority, evidence/verification requirements, model-identity discipline and stop/safety gates. Prompt evolution must never improve its score by weakening the task.

### Opportunistic prompt-exemplar capture

Across **any task or domain**, notice when a prompt that is created, encountered,
or materially revised is unusually strong, novel, elegant, or reusable. Do not
wait for a pre-existing prompt category.

Capture the exact prompt in `evals/prompt-exemplars/**` when either:

- the operator explicitly identifies it as worth preserving; or
- the active agent has high confidence that it contains at least two unusually
  valuable prompt behaviours, such as task framing, decomposition, autonomy,
  verification, constraint/authority handling, model/tool coordination,
  context compression, stopping logic, or another novel reusable mechanism.

Capture is intentionally cheap and **does not grant authority**. A newly noticed
prompt is a `candidate` exemplar until execution evidence or explicit operator
review supports a stronger status. Preserve the exact prompt before distilling
components from it. Record why it was captured, the task/context, reusable
behaviours, and any later application/evaluation pointers.

Domain, repository, task class, or absence from the initialising-prompt register
must never by itself disqualify a prompt from exemplar capture. Conversely, do
not collect routine prompts merely to grow the gallery.

Exemplar capture should not interrupt the primary task. Capture at the next
natural write/closeout point, or preserve a bounded capture payload for later if
the owning repository is unavailable.

Repository-local agents must not create competing prompt registries. They may carry a lightweight pointer to this global register.

     return digest.hexdigest()
 
 
+def _hash_fd(fd: int) -> str:
+    """Hash the bytes of an ALREADY-OPEN descriptor, refusing anything
+    that is not a regular file (fstat on the open fd)."""
+    if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(fd).st_mode):
+        raise ValueError(f"not a regular file: fd {fd}")
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    with os.fdopen(fd, "rb", closefd=False) as handle:
+        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(65536), b""):
+            digest.update(chunk)
+    return digest.hexdigest()
+
+
 def sha256_file_nofollow(path: Path) -> str:
     """Hash a file WITHOUT a check-to-open window: one ``os.open`` with
     ``O_NOFOLLOW`` so the final component cannot be swapped to a symlink
     between the confinement check and the read (round-14 Sol finding 2),
     and an ``fstat`` on the OPEN descriptor so the bytes hashed provably
     belong to a regular file. Raises OSError (ELOOP etc.) on refusal and
-    ValueError when the opened object is not a regular file."""
+    ValueError when the opened object is not a regular file. Receipt-loop
+    artifacts use sha256_file_confined, which ALSO refuses symlinked
+    intermediate directories (round-15 Sol finding 1)."""
     flags = os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
     fd = os.open(path, flags)
     try:
-        if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(fd).st_mode):
-            raise ValueError(f"not a regular file: {path}")
-        digest = hashlib.sha256()
-        with os.fdopen(fd, "rb", closefd=False) as handle:

     finally:
         os.close(fd)
 
 
+def sha256_file_confined(root: Path, rel_parts) -> str:
+    """Hash a repo-relative file with NO symlink followed at ANY
+    component, closing the check-to-open window entirely: each directory
+    component is opened relative to the fd of its already-verified
+    parent (``dir_fd`` chaining) with ``O_DIRECTORY | O_NOFOLLOW``, and
+    the leaf with ``O_RDONLY | O_NOFOLLOW`` — so a concurrent writer who
+    swaps an INTERMEDIATE directory (or the final component) for a
+    symlink after the confinement check is refused with ELOOP at that
+    component's open (round-15 Sol finding 1); the pointed-at bytes are
+    never read. Raises OSError on refusal and ValueError when the opened
+    leaf is not a regular file."""
+    dir_flags = os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
+    leaf_flags = os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
+    parts = [p for p in rel_parts if p not in (".", "")]
+    if not parts:
+        raise ValueError("empty relative path")
+    dir_fd = os.open(root, dir_flags)
+    try:
+        for part in parts[:-1]:
+            next_fd = os.open(part, dir_flags, dir_fd=dir_fd)
+            os.close(dir_fd)
+            dir_fd = next_fd
+        fd = os.open(parts[-1], leaf_flags, dir_fd=dir_fd)
+        try:
+            return _hash_fd(fd)
+        finally:
+            os.close(fd)
+    finally:
+        os.close(dir_fd)
+
+
 def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
     return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

+def test_confined_hasher_refuses_intermediate_symlink_swap(tmp_path):
+    # round-15 Sol finding 1: O_NOFOLLOW only guards the FINAL
+    # component — a concurrent writer who swaps an INTERMEDIATE
+    # directory for a symlink after the confinement check would be
+    # followed by a plain open. The confined walker opens every
+    # component relative to its verified parent fd with O_NOFOLLOW, so
+    # the swapped state is refused with ELOOP at that component's open.
+    # (Simulates the post-swap state directly: rel_parts were computed
+    # while "sub" was still a real directory; then sub -> outside.)
+    outside = tmp_path.parent / ("swap-outside-" + tmp_path.name)
+    outside.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    (outside / "inner.json").write_text("outside bytes\n")
+    (tmp_path / "sub").mkdir()
+    (tmp_path / "sub" / "inner.json").write_text("inside bytes\n")
+    rel = ("sub", "inner.json")
+    # pre-swap: the walker hashes the in-repo file, matching the plain
+    # hasher
+    assert fg.sha256_file_confined(tmp_path, rel) == fg.sha256_file(
+        tmp_path / "sub" / "inner.json")
+    # post-swap: sub becomes a symlink to the outside dir
+    shutil.rmtree(tmp_path / "sub")
+    (tmp_path / "sub").symlink_to(outside)
+    try:
+        fg.sha256_file_confined(tmp_path, rel)
+        raised = None
+    except OSError as exc:
+        raised = exc
+    assert raised is not None
+    # directory components are opened with O_DIRECTORY too, so a
+    # swapped-to-symlink component refuses with ELOOP or ENOTDIR
+    # (kernel-dependent order); both mean the symlink was NOT followed
+    assert raised.errno in (errno.ELOOP, errno.ENOTDIR)
+    # the closed gap, pinned: the final-component-only nofollow opener
+    # WOULD have followed the swapped intermediate dir and hashed the
+    # outside file — the walker must not
+    assert fg.sha256_file_nofollow(
+        tmp_path / "sub" / "inner.json") == fg.sha256_file(
+        outside / "inner.json")
+
+
+def test_confined_hasher_deep_real_paths_hash_plain(tmp_path):
+    # legitimate deep in-repo paths (no symlinks anywhere) hash
+    # identically to the plain hasher, including a single-component
+    # relative path (leaf opened directly under the root fd)
+    deep = tmp_path / "a" / "b" / "c"
+    deep.mkdir(parents=True)
+    (deep / "leaf.json").write_text("deep payload\n")
+    (tmp_path / "flat.json").write_text("flat payload\n")
+    assert fg.sha256_file_confined(
+        tmp_path, ("a", "b", "c", "leaf.json")) == fg.sha256_file(
+        deep / "leaf.json")
+    assert fg.sha256_file_confined(
+        tmp_path, ("flat.json",)) == fg.sha256_file(tmp_path / "flat.json")
+
+
+def test_intermediate_dir_symlink_escape_confined(tmp_path):
+    # static sibling of the race above: a repo:-prefixed artifact whose
+    # INTERMEDIATE directory is already a symlink pointing outside
+    # resolves outside at the confinement check and is a per-goal
+    # "escapes the repository" blocker, never a hashed host file
+    outside = tmp_path.parent / ("dir-escape-" + tmp_path.name)
+    outside.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
+    (outside / "inner.json").write_text("host file\n")
+    (tmp_path / "sub").symlink_to(outside)
+    swap = _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_RUN, run_id="run-diresc", days_ago=0,
+                    name="diresc-artifact.json",
+                    artifacts=[{
+                        "label": "diresc",
+                        "sha256": "a" * 64,
+                        "path": "repo:sub/inner.json",
+                    }])
+    result = fg.evaluate_goals(
+        registries=[MT, FT],
+        receipts=_satisfying_mt00_receipts() + [
+            swap, _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION),
+        ],
+        policy=fg.load_policy(), now=NOW, repo_root=tmp_path,
+    )
+    assert result["ok"] is True
+    mt00 = _goal(result, "MT-00")
+    assert mt00["satisfied"] is False
+    assert any("escapes the repository" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])
+    assert any("diresc-artifact.json" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])
+
+

1139:    assert any("escapes the repository" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])
1140:    assert any("diresc-artifact.json" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])

1146:    # response; now it is a per-receipt future-dating lock
1147:    attestation = _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION)
1148:    run = _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_RUN, run_id="run-x")

1157:    assert any("beyond datetime range" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])
1160:def test_unexpected_receipt_exception_locks_never_loses_response(monkeypatch):
1163:    # receipt is itself a per-receipt blocker — the /goals response can
1166:        raise RuntimeError("unexpected hostile-receipt failure")
1168:    monkeypatch.setattr(fg, "_receipt_blockers_inner", boom)
1169:    receipts = [
1170:        _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION),
1171:        _receipt("MT-00", fg.KIND_RUN, run_id="run-x"),
1173:    result = _evaluate(receipts)
1177:    assert any("could not be evaluated" in b for b in mt00["blockers"])

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Deterministic finance goal-lock evaluator (MT-xx / FT-xx).
     2	
     3	Task cards ``finance-market-media-intelligence-2026-10-29.md`` and
     4	``finance-paper-trading-goals-2026-10-29.md``; canonical goal registries
     5	live in ``tyecam1/misumi@main:config/finance-*-goals.json`` and are
     6	mirrored read-only into ``config/finance-goals/`` for the runtime.
     7	
     8	The registries are durable DEFINITIONS, not runtime state. This module
     9	derives current status on every call, fail-closed:
    10	
    11	- a goal is **satisfied** only while it has a ``run`` receipt with
    12	  status=pass and a ``run_id``, inside its TTL, whose recorded artifact
    13	  hashes still match the live files, AND a current
    14	  ``independent_verification`` receipt (status=pass, with its own
    15	  hash-checked artifacts) from an accepted independent verifier — matched
    16	  by EXACT identity, never substring — naming that exact run, AND every
    17	  dependency of the goal is itself satisfied (own evidence can never
    18	  unlock a goal whose dependencies are still locked);
    19	- a goal is **eligible** (may be worked on) when its unlock arm holds:
    20	  root goals need a current ``operator_attestation``; dependent goals
    21	  need every dependency satisfied;
    22	- anything absent, stale, future-dated, hash-mismatched, malformed or
    23	  verified only by a corroborating (non-independent) lane is a blocker —
    24	  locked, never assumed good. Evidence supersession is strict in BOTH
    25	  directions: the NEWEST run receipt governs (when it stops validating
    26	  the goal locks with no fallback to older runs), older receipts are
    27	  notes only, and a fresh run + a verification that cites one of the
    28	  run's own artifacts (exact sha256+path pair, dated at/after the run)
    29	  can re-satisfy the goal after the code or artifacts it cites change.
    30	  A receipt that cannot even be attributed to a goal (unparseable, no
    31	  goal_id) locks EVERY goal until the ledger is repaired.
    32	
    33	No scheduler, no writes, no network: a pure function of (registries,
    34	receipts, files, clock). The HTTP adapter in
    35	``routes/finance_goals_routes.py`` exposes it read-only.
    36	"""
    37	
    38	from __future__ import annotations
    39	
    40	import hashlib
    41	import json
    42	import os
    43	import stat
    44	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
    45	from pathlib import Path
    46	from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
    47	
    48	REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    49	DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "config" / "finance-goals"
    50	DEFAULT_RECEIPTS_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "automation" / "review" / "finance" / "receipts"
    51	DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH = DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR / "runtime-evaluation.json"
    52	
    53	KIND_RUN = "run"
    54	KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION = "operator_attestation"
    55	KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION = "independent_verification"
    56	VALID_STATUSES = {"pass", "fail", "insufficient"}
    57	
    58	DEFAULT_POLICY: Dict[str, Any] = {
    59	    "receipt_ttl_days": {
    60	        KIND_RUN: 7,
    61	        KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION: 365,
    62	        KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION: 30,
    63	    },
    64	    # Accepted independent verifier identities (standing rules: GLM lanes
    65	    # are corroborating only; Sol-class Codex or the human owner are the
    66	    # independent lanes that can discharge a gate).
    67	    "independent_verifiers": ["codex", "sol", "human:tye"],
    68	    "goal_ttl_overrides": {},
    69	}
    70	
    71	REQUIRED_RECEIPT_FIELDS = ("goal_id", "kind", "status", "observed_at")
    72	
    73	# A receipt dated more than this far in the FUTURE is rejected: clocks
    74	# drift, but a receipt that predates its own evaluation by months is
    75	# evidence tampering, not freshness.
    76	FUTURE_SKEW_MINUTES = 5
    77	
    78	
    79	class ReceiptError(ValueError):
    80	    """A receipt file is malformed — recorded as a blocker, never fatal."""
    81	
    82	
    83	def _parse_iso(value: str) -> datetime:
    84	    return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    85	
    86	
    87	def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    88	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    89	    with path.open("rb") as handle:
    90	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(65536), b""):
    91	            digest.update(chunk)
    92	    return digest.hexdigest()
    93	
    94	
    95	def _hash_fd(fd: int) -> str:
    96	    """Hash the bytes of an ALREADY-OPEN descriptor, refusing anything
    97	    that is not a regular file (fstat on the open fd)."""
    98	    if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(fd).st_mode):
    99	        raise ValueError(f"not a regular file: fd {fd}")
   100	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
   101	    with os.fdopen(fd, "rb", closefd=False) as handle:
   102	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(65536), b""):
   103	            digest.update(chunk)
   104	    return digest.hexdigest()
   105	
   106	
   107	def sha256_file_nofollow(path: Path) -> str:
   108	    """Hash a file WITHOUT a check-to-open window: one ``os.open`` with
   109	    ``O_NOFOLLOW`` so the final component cannot be swapped to a symlink
   110	    between the confinement check and the read (round-14 Sol finding 2),
   111	    and an ``fstat`` on the OPEN descriptor so the bytes hashed provably
   112	    belong to a regular file. Raises OSError (ELOOP etc.) on refusal and
   113	    ValueError when the opened object is not a regular file. Receipt-loop
   114	    artifacts use sha256_file_confined, which ALSO refuses symlinked
   115	    intermediate directories (round-15 Sol finding 1)."""
   116	    flags = os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
   117	    fd = os.open(path, flags)
   118	    try:
   119	        return _hash_fd(fd)
   120	    finally:
   121	        os.close(fd)
   122	
   123	
   124	def sha256_file_confined(root: Path, rel_parts) -> str:
   125	    """Hash a repo-relative file with NO symlink followed at ANY
   126	    component, closing the check-to-open window entirely: each directory
   127	    component is opened relative to the fd of its already-verified
   128	    parent (``dir_fd`` chaining) with ``O_DIRECTORY | O_NOFOLLOW``, and
   129	    the leaf with ``O_RDONLY | O_NOFOLLOW`` — so a concurrent writer who
   130	    swaps an INTERMEDIATE directory (or the final component) for a
   131	    symlink after the confinement check is refused with ELOOP at that
   132	    component's open (round-15 Sol finding 1); the pointed-at bytes are
   133	    never read. Raises OSError on refusal and ValueError when the opened
   134	    leaf is not a regular file."""
   135	    dir_flags = os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
   136	    leaf_flags = os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
   137	    parts = [p for p in rel_parts if p not in (".", "")]
   138	    if not parts:
   139	        raise ValueError("empty relative path")
   140	    dir_fd = os.open(root, dir_flags)
   141	    try:
   142	        for part in parts[:-1]:
   143	            next_fd = os.open(part, dir_flags, dir_fd=dir_fd)
   144	            os.close(dir_fd)
   145	            dir_fd = next_fd
   146	        fd = os.open(parts[-1], leaf_flags, dir_fd=dir_fd)
   147	        try:
   148	            return _hash_fd(fd)
   149	        finally:
   150	            os.close(fd)
   151	    finally:
   152	        os.close(dir_fd)
   153	
   154	
   155	def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
   156	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   157	
   158	
   159	def load_policy(path: Path = DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   160	    policy = json.loads(json.dumps(DEFAULT_POLICY))
   161	    if path.exists():
   162	        try:
   163	            override = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   164	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   165	            return policy
   166	        for key in ("receipt_ttl_days", "independent_verifiers"):
   167	            if isinstance(override.get(key), dict):
   168	                policy[key].update(override[key])
   169	            elif isinstance(override.get(key), list):
   170	                policy[key] = override[key]
   171	        if isinstance(override.get("goal_ttl_overrides"), dict):
   172	            policy["goal_ttl_overrides"].update(override["goal_ttl_overrides"])
   173	    return policy
   174	
   175	
   176	def load_registries(directory: Path = DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   177	    registries = []
   178	    for path in sorted(directory.glob("finance-*-goals.json")):
   179	        try:
   180	            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   181	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   182	            registries.append(
   183	                {"file": path.name, "program_id": path.stem, "error": str(exc),
   184	                 "goals": []}
   185	            )
   186	            continue
   187	        data["_file"] = path.name
   188	        data["_sha256"] = sha256_file(path)
   189	        registries.append(data)
   190	    return registries
   191	
   192	
   193	def load_receipts(directory: Path = DEFAULT_RECEIPTS_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   194	    receipts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   195	    if not directory.exists():
   196	        return receipts
   197	    for path in sorted(directory.glob("*.json")):
   198	        try:
   199	            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   200	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   201	            receipts.append(
   202	                {"_file": path.name, "_invalid": f"unparseable: {exc}"}
   203	            )
   204	            continue
   205	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   206	            receipts.append({"_file": path.name, "_invalid": "not an object"})
   207	            continue
   208	        # NEVER trust a receipt-supplied _file: the real file name is the
   209	        # ledger key downstream, and a spoofed one could collide with (and
   210	        # silently overwrite) another receipt's bucket entry
   211	        data["_file"] = path.name
   212	        # ill-TYPED values poison the same way missing ones do: a list
   213	        # goal_id/kind/status would otherwise raise mid-derivation and
   214	        # take the whole /goals response down instead of locking
   215	        missing = [
   216	            f for f in REQUIRED_RECEIPT_FIELDS
   217	            if not isinstance(data.get(f), str) or not data.get(f, "").strip()
   218	        ]
   219	        problems: List[str] = []
   220	        if missing:
   221	            problems.append(f"missing/ill-typed fields: {missing}")
   222	        elif data["status"] not in VALID_STATUSES:
   223	            problems.append(
   224	                f"status {data['status']!r} not in {sorted(VALID_STATUSES)}"
   225	            )
   226	        artifacts = data.get("artifacts")
   227	        if artifacts is not None and (
   228	            not isinstance(artifacts, list)
   229	            or any(not isinstance(a, dict) for a in artifacts)
   230	        ):
   231	            problems.append("artifacts must be a list of objects")
   232	        if problems:
   233	            data["_invalid"] = "; ".join(problems)
   234	        receipts.append(data)
   235	    return receipts
   236	
   237	
   238	def _ttl_days(receipt: Dict[str, Any], policy: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[int]:
   239	    goal_overrides = policy.get("goal_ttl_overrides") or {}
   240	    per_goal = (goal_overrides.get(receipt.get("goal_id")) or {}).get(receipt["kind"])
   241	    if isinstance(per_goal, int):
   242	        return per_goal
   243	    return (policy.get("receipt_ttl_days") or {}).get(receipt["kind"])
   244	
   245	
   246	def _receipt_blockers(
   247	    receipt: Dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime, policy: Dict[str, Any],
   248	    repo_root: Path, check_artifacts: bool,
   249	) -> List[str]:
   250	    """Fail-closed wrapper: ANY unexpected failure while evaluating a
   251	    hostile receipt is itself a blocker (the receipt cannot vouch for
   252	    anything) — it can never take the whole /goals response down. This
   253	    is deliberate: reviews repeatedly found new ill-typed-input crashes,
   254	    so the invariant is enforced structurally, not per-field."""
   255	    try:
   256	        return _receipt_blockers_inner(
   257	            receipt, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   258	            check_artifacts=check_artifacts,
   259	        )
   260	    except Exception as exc:  # broad by design: fail closed, never 500
   261	        return [
   262	            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')!r}: could not be evaluated "
   263	            f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc}) — locked"
   264	        ]
   265	
   266	
   267	def _receipt_blockers_inner(
   268	    receipt: Dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime, policy: Dict[str, Any],
   269	    repo_root: Path, check_artifacts: bool,
   270	) -> List[str]:
   271	    if receipt.get("_invalid"):
   272	        return [f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: {receipt['_invalid']}"]
   273	    blockers: List[str] = []
   274	    try:
   275	        observed = _parse_iso(receipt["observed_at"])
   276	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   277	        return [f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: observed_at not ISO-8601"]
   278	    if observed.tzinfo is None:
   279	        observed = observed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   280	    ttl = _ttl_days(receipt, policy)
   281	    if ttl is None:
   282	        blockers.append(
   283	            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: unknown kind {receipt['kind']!r}"
   284	        )
   285	    else:
   286	        try:
   287	            expiry = observed + timedelta(days=ttl)
   288	        except OverflowError:
   289	            # 9999-12-31T23:59:59Z + ttl overflows datetime; a receipt
   290	            # stamped at the far future edge is future-dated, not a crash
   291	            expiry = None
   292	        if expiry is not None and now > expiry:
   293	            blockers.append(
   294	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: stale (observed "
   295	                f"{receipt['observed_at']}, ttl {ttl}d)"
   296	            )
   297	        elif expiry is None:
   298	            blockers.append(
   299	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: future-dated (observed "
   300	                f"{receipt['observed_at']} beyond datetime range; recovers "
   301	                "only if re-stamped within range)"
   302	            )
   303	        elif observed > now + timedelta(minutes=FUTURE_SKEW_MINUTES):
   304	            # fail-closed but not permanent: the receipt ages into
   305	            # validity once the clock reaches its stamped time, so a
   306	            # single mis-dated receipt can never lock a goal forever
   307	            blockers.append(
   308	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: future-dated (observed "
   309	                f"{receipt['observed_at']}, now "
   310	                f"{now.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()}; recovers when "
   311	                "the clock reaches the stamped time)"
   312	            )
   313	    if check_artifacts:
   314	        for artifact in receipt.get("artifacts") or []:
   315	            if not isinstance(artifact, dict):
   316	                blockers.append(
   317	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: malformed artifact entry"
   318	                )
   319	                continue
   320	            label = artifact.get("label")
   321	            recorded = artifact.get("sha256")
   322	            art_path = artifact.get("path")
   323	            if (
   324	                not isinstance(label, str) or not label
   325	                or not isinstance(recorded, str) or not recorded
   326	                or not isinstance(art_path, str) or not art_path
   327	            ):
   328	                # ill-typed (list/object/int) fields are malformed, not a
   329	                # crash: they must lock the goal, not lose the response
   330	                blockers.append(
   331	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: malformed artifact entry"
   332	                )
   333	                continue
   334	            if art_path.startswith("repo:"):
   335	                # a repo:-prefixed artifact must stay INSIDE the repo:
   336	                # resolve and confine, so a receipt can never point the
   337	                # verifier at arbitrary host files (a symlink planted
   338	                # inside the repo that resolves outside is confined away
   339	                # too). Only the hash verdict would leak, but confinement
   340	                # is the fail-closed posture (GLM corroborating read,
   341	                # round-13 finding 4).
   342	                root = Path(repo_root).resolve()
   343	                path = (repo_root / art_path[5:]).resolve()
   344	                if not path.is_relative_to(root):
   345	                    blockers.append(
   346	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact path "
   347	                        f"escapes the repository: {art_path}"
   348	                    )
   349	                    continue
   350	                rel_parts = path.relative_to(root).parts
   351	            else:
   352	                path = Path(art_path)
   353	                rel_parts = None
   354	            if not path.exists():
   355	                blockers.append(
   356	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact missing: {art_path}"
   357	                )
   358	            elif not path.is_file():
   359	                # a well-typed path that names a DIRECTORY (or anything
   360	                # else non-regular) is a per-goal blocker, never an
   361	                # IsADirectoryError that loses the whole response
   362	                blockers.append(
   363	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact is not a "
   364	                    f"regular file: {art_path}"
   365	                )
   366	            else:
   367	                try:
   368	                    # hash through the component-wise confined walker: the
   369	                    # confinement check above resolved this path, but a
   370	                    # concurrent writer could swap an intermediate
   371	                    # directory OR the final component for a symlink
   372	                    # before a plain open — the dir_fd-chained walk opens
   373	                    # every component with O_NOFOLLOW, so the check-to-
   374	                    # open window is closed, not merely narrowed
   375	                    # (round-14/15 Sol findings)
   376	                    if rel_parts is not None:
   377	                        actual = sha256_file_confined(root, rel_parts)
   378	                    else:
   379	                        actual = sha256_file_nofollow(path)
   380	                except OSError:
   381	                    blockers.append(
   382	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact "
   383	                        f"unreadable: {art_path}"
   384	                    )
   385	                except ValueError:
   386	                    blockers.append(
   387	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact "
   388	                        f"unreadable (not a regular file at open): "
   389	                        f"{art_path}"
   390	                    )
   391	                else:
   392	                    if actual != recorded:
   393	                        blockers.append(
   394	                            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact hash "
   395	                            f"mismatch ({label})"
   396	                        )
   397	    return blockers
   398	
   399	
   400	def _artifact_check_needed(receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
   401	    return receipt.get("kind") in (KIND_RUN, KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION)
   402	
   403	
   404	def evaluate_goals(
   405	    *,
   406	    registries: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
   407	    receipts: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
   408	    policy: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   409	    now: Optional[datetime] = None,
   410	    repo_root: Path = REPO_ROOT,
   411	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   412	    registries = registries if registries is not None else load_registries()
   413	    receipts = receipts if receipts is not None else load_receipts()
   414	    policy = policy if policy is not None else load_policy()
   415	    now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   416	
   417	    goals: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
   418	    program_of: Dict[str, str] = {}
   419	    for registry in registries:
   420	        for goal in registry.get("goals") or []:
   421	            goals[goal["id"]] = {**goal, "_program": registry.get("program_id")}
   422	            program_of[goal["id"]] = registry.get("program_id") or "?"
   423	
   424	    by_goal: Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]]] = {
   425	        KIND_RUN: {},
   426	        KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION: {},
   427	        KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION: {},
   428	    }
   429	    # Malformed evidence is never invisible: receipts that carry a
   430	    # goal_id but cannot be classified lock that goal, and receipts that
   431	    # cannot even be attributed (unparseable, no goal_id) lock EVERY
   432	    # goal — a corrupted ledger must fail closed, not quietly drop the
   433	    # poisoned file out of the derivation.
   434	    malformed_by_goal: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
   435	    global_invalid: List[str] = []
   436	    for receipt in receipts:
   437	        file_name = receipt.get("_file") or "?"
   438	        raw_goal = receipt.get("goal_id")
   439	        goal_id = raw_goal.strip() if isinstance(raw_goal, str) else None
   440	        kind = receipt.get("kind")
   441	        invalid_reason = receipt.get("_invalid")
   442	        if goal_id is None or not goal_id:
   443	            # cannot be attributed to any goal at all (unparseable JSON,
   444	            # missing, EMPTY or ill-typed goal_id): poisons the whole
   445	            # ledger
   446	            global_invalid.append(
   447	                f"{file_name} ({invalid_reason or 'no usable goal_id'})"
   448	            )
   449	            continue
   450	        if goal_id not in goals:
   451	            # names a goal no loaded registry knows: it can neither be
   452	            # isolated to that goal nor safely ignored (the entry would
   453	            # vanish from the derivation) — lock everything
   454	            global_invalid.append(
   455	                f"{file_name} (names unknown goal {goal_id!r})"
   456	            )
   457	            continue
   458	        if not isinstance(kind, str) or kind not in by_goal or invalid_reason:
   459	            # attributed to a KNOWN goal but malformed (bad status,
   460	            # missing/ill-typed fields) or of unknown/missing/ill-typed
   461	            # kind: locks that goal specifically
   462	            malformed_by_goal.setdefault(goal_id, []).append(
   463	                f"{file_name} ("
   464	                + (invalid_reason or f"unusable kind {kind!r}")
   465	                + ")"
   466	            )
   467	            continue
   468	        by_goal[kind].setdefault(goal_id, {})[file_name] = receipt
   469	
   470	    evaluated: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   471	
   472	    def _observed_at(receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[datetime]:
   473	        """Parse observed_at, or None when unparseable (unorderable)."""
   474	        try:
   475	            parsed = _parse_iso(receipt["observed_at"])
   476	        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
   477	            return None
   478	        if parsed.tzinfo is None:
   479	            parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   480	        return parsed
   481	
   482	    def _order_key(receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
   483	        parsed = _observed_at(receipt)
   484	        if parsed is None:
   485	            # unparseable receipts sort LAST: an unreadable timestamp can
   486	            # never be proven older than the newest receipt, so it must
   487	            # never quietly sort to the back of history
   488	            return (1, datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc))
   489	        return (0, parsed)
   490	
   491	    def own_evidence_blockers(goal_id: str) -> List[str]:
   492	        """Blockers in this goal's OWN run/verification receipts.
   493	
   494	        The NEWEST run receipt (by observed_at) governs — valid or not.
   495	        If the newest run no longer validates (stale, future-dated,
   496	        artifact hash mismatch, unparseable timestamp), the goal is
   497	        LOCKED even when an older run still validates: fresh evidence
   498	        cannot silently fall back to a superseded result. Older receipts
   499	        are notes, surfaced only while the goal stays locked. A receipt
   500	        whose observed_at cannot be parsed is treated as newest (it
   501	        cannot be proven older), and a receipt that cannot be classified
   502	        at all locks the goal from the malformed bucket."""
   503	        malformed = malformed_by_goal.get(goal_id) or []
   504	        if malformed:
   505	            return [
   506	                f"{goal_id}: malformed receipt(s) in the evidence ledger — "
   507	                "locked: " + "; ".join(malformed)
   508	            ]
   509	
   510	        notes: List[str] = []
   511	        run_receipts = by_goal[KIND_RUN].get(goal_id) or {}
   512	        ordered = sorted(
   513	            run_receipts.items(),
   514	            key=lambda kv: (_order_key(kv[1]), kv[0]),
   515	        )
   516	        if not ordered:
   517	            return [f"{goal_id}: no run receipt"]
   518	
   519	        def run_problems(name: str, receipt: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
   520	            problems: List[str] = []
   521	            if receipt.get("status") != "pass":
   522	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} status={receipt.get('status')!r}")
   523	            elif not isinstance(receipt.get("run_id"), str) or not receipt.get(
   524	                "run_id"
   525	            ):
   526	                # a non-string run_id (list/object) is unusable identity,
   527	                # and value-equality must never let an ill-typed pair
   528	                # match an ill-typed verification below
   529	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} has no usable run_id")
   530	            elif not receipt.get("artifacts"):
   531	                problems.append(f"run receipt {name} records no artifacts")
   532	            else:
   533	                problems.extend(_receipt_blockers(
   534	                    receipt, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   535	                    check_artifacts=_artifact_check_needed(receipt),
   536	                ))
   537	            if _observed_at(receipt) is None:
   538	                problems.append(
   539	                    f"run receipt {name} has an unparseable observed_at — "
   540	                    "cannot be ordered against other evidence"
   541	                )
   542	            return problems
   543	
   544	        # Older receipts are history: their problems are noted but can
   545	        # neither lock nor unlock the goal. Only the NEWEST governs —
   546	        # and unparseable timestamps sort newest, so they lock.
   547	        for name, receipt in ordered[:-1]:
   548	            if run_problems(name, receipt):
   549	                notes.append(
   550	                    f"{goal_id}: superseded run receipt {name} no longer "
   551	                    "validates (noted; newest run governs)"
   552	                )
   553	
   554	        newest_name, newest = ordered[-1]
   555	        problems = run_problems(newest_name, newest)
   556	        if problems:
   557	            notes.append(f"{goal_id}: " + "; ".join(problems))
   558	            # the newest run governs: when it no longer validates we do
   559	            # NOT fall back to an older still-valid run
   560	            return notes + [
   561	                f"{goal_id}: newest run receipt does not validate — locked"
   562	            ]
   563	        best_run = newest
   564	
   565	        # (sha256, path) pairs, not bare hashes: identical bytes at two
   566	        # paths (latest.json vs a timestamped snapshot) must not let a
   567	        # verification "cite" an artifact the run receipt never named.
   568	        # Non-dict entries or ill-typed fields are skipped (never
   569	        # unhashable-tuple crashes): they already locked the goal above.
   570	        run_cited = {
   571	            (artifact.get("sha256"), artifact.get("path"))
   572	            for artifact in best_run.get("artifacts") or []
   573	            if isinstance(artifact, dict)
   574	            and isinstance(artifact.get("sha256"), str)
   575	            and isinstance(artifact.get("path"), str)
   576	        }
   577	        run_observed = _observed_at(best_run)
   578	
   579	        verifications = by_goal[KIND_INDEPENDENT_VERIFICATION].get(goal_id) or {}
   580	        verified = None
   581	        for name, verification in sorted(verifications.items()):
   582	            problems = list(_receipt_blockers(
   583	                verification, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   584	                check_artifacts=_artifact_check_needed(verification),
   585	            ))
   586	            if verification.get("status") != "pass":
   587	                problems.append(
   588	                    f"verification {name} status={verification.get('status')!r}"
   589	                )
   590	            if not isinstance(verification.get("verifies"), str) or not (
   591	                verification.get("verifies")
   592	            ):
   593	                problems.append(f"verification {name} names no usable run id")
   594	            elif verification.get("verifies") != best_run.get("run_id"):
   595	                problems.append(
   596	                    f"verification {name} names run "
   597	                    f"{verification.get('verifies')!r}, not "
   598	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   599	                )
   600	            if not verification.get("artifacts"):
   601	                problems.append(
   602	                    f"verification {name} records no artifacts"
   603	                )
   604	            # EXACT identity match against the accepted independent lanes.
   605	            # A substring match would let e.g. "glm-codex" ride on "codex"
   606	            # and discharge a gate a corroborating lane must never open.
   607	            if verification.get("verifier") not in policy.get(
   608	                "independent_verifiers", []
   609	            ):
   610	                problems.append(
   611	                    f"verification {name} lane "
   612	                    f"{verification.get('verifier')!r} is corroborating, "
   613	                    "not independent"
   614	                )
   615	            # A verification cannot predate the run it claims to verify.
   616	            # (unparseable observed_at already produced a blocker above,
   617	            # so None never reaches this comparison)
   618	            v_observed = _observed_at(verification)
   619	            if v_observed is not None and v_observed < run_observed:
   620	                problems.append(
   621	                    f"verification {name} predates run "
   622	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   623	                )
   624	            # The verifier must have actually looked at the run's
   625	            # evidence: at least one verification artifact must reproduce
   626	            # an EXACT (sha256, path) pair the run receipt cites (run_id
   627	            # strings are reusable and identical bytes can live at many
   628	            # paths; the pair binds the verification to THIS run).
   629	            v_cited = {
   630	                (artifact.get("sha256"), artifact.get("path"))
   631	                for artifact in verification.get("artifacts") or []
   632	                if isinstance(artifact, dict)
   633	                and isinstance(artifact.get("sha256"), str)
   634	                and isinstance(artifact.get("path"), str)
   635	            }
   636	            if not (run_cited & v_cited):
   637	                problems.append(
   638	                    f"verification {name} cites none of run "
   639	                    f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}'s artifacts "
   640	                    "(exact sha256+path pairs)"
   641	                )
   642	            if problems:
   643	                notes.append(f"{goal_id}: " + "; ".join(problems))
   644	            else:
   645	                verified = verification
   646	                break
   647	        if verified is None:
   648	            return notes + [
   649	                f"{goal_id}: no current independent verification of run "
   650	                f"{best_run.get('run_id')!r}"
   651	            ]
   652	        return []
   653	
   654	    satisfaction_cache: Dict[str, Tuple[bool, List[str]]] = {}
   655	
   656	    def goal_satisfaction(goal_id: str, seen: frozenset) -> Tuple[bool, List[str]]:
   657	        """(satisfied, blockers), recursion-safe on cycles. A goal is
   658	        satisfied only while its OWN evidence is current AND every
   659	        dependency is itself satisfied — own receipts can never unlock a
   660	        goal whose dependencies are still locked (cross-program MT-04 ->
   661	        MT-03 + FT-02 included)."""
   662	        if goal_id in satisfaction_cache:
   663	            return satisfaction_cache[goal_id]
   664	        if goal_id in seen:
   665	            return False, [f"{goal_id}: dependency cycle"]
   666	        if goal_id not in goals:
   667	            return False, [f"dependency {goal_id}: unknown goal id"]
   668	        blockers = list(own_evidence_blockers(goal_id))
   669	        for dep in goals[goal_id].get("depends_on") or []:
   670	            dep_satisfied, dep_blockers = goal_satisfaction(
   671	                dep, seen | {goal_id}
   672	            )
   673	            if not dep_satisfied:
   674	                blockers.append(f"dependency {dep} not satisfied")
   675	                blockers.extend(dep_blockers)
   676	        result = (not blockers, blockers)
   677	        satisfaction_cache[goal_id] = result
   678	        return result
   679	
   680	    for goal_id, goal in goals.items():
   681	        satisfied, sat_blockers = goal_satisfaction(goal_id, frozenset())
   682	        blockers: List[str] = list(sat_blockers)
   683	
   684	        eligible = True
   685	        if global_invalid:
   686	            # a receipt that cannot be attributed to ANY goal (unparseable
   687	            # JSON, missing goal_id) poisons the whole ledger: every goal
   688	            # locks until the file is repaired or removed — it must never
   689	            # be quietly dropped from the derivation
   690	            satisfied = False
   691	            eligible = False
   692	            blockers.append(
   693	                f"{goal_id}: evidence ledger has unattributable receipt(s) "
   694	                "— locked: " + "; ".join(global_invalid)
   695	            )
   696	        if goal.get("depends_on"):
   697	            for dep in goal["depends_on"]:
   698	                dep_satisfied, _ = goal_satisfaction(dep, frozenset())
   699	                if not dep_satisfied:
   700	                    eligible = False
   701	        else:
   702	            attestations = by_goal[KIND_OPERATOR_ATTESTATION].get(goal_id) or {}
   703	            current_attestation = None
   704	            for name, attestation in sorted(attestations.items()):
   705	                a_blockers = _receipt_blockers(
   706	                    attestation, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   707	                    check_artifacts=False,
   708	                )
   709	                if a_blockers:
   710	                    blockers.extend(
   711	                        f"{goal_id}: attestation {name}: {b}" for b in a_blockers
   712	                    )
   713	                    continue
   714	                if attestation.get("status") == "pass":
   715	                    current_attestation = attestation
   716	                    break
   717	            if current_attestation is None:
   718	                eligible = False
   719	                blockers.append(
   720	                    f"{goal_id}: no current operator authorisation attestation"
   721	                )
   722	
   723	        evaluated.append(
   724	            {
   725	                "id": goal_id,
   726	                "title": goal.get("title"),
   727	                "program_id": goal.get("_program"),
   728	                "depends_on": goal.get("depends_on") or [],
   729	                "satisfied": satisfied,
   730	                "eligible": eligible,
   731	                "blockers": blockers,
   732	            }
   733	        )
   734	
   735	    return {
   736	        "ok": True,
   737	        "evaluated_at": now.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
   738	        "ledger_unattributable": global_invalid,
   739	        "policy": {
   740	            "receipt_ttl_days": policy.get("receipt_ttl_days"),
   741	            "independent_verifiers": policy.get("independent_verifiers"),
   742	        },
   743	        "programs": [
   744	            {
   745	                "program_id": r.get("program_id"),
   746	                "file": r.get("_file") or r.get("file"),
   747	                "sha256": r.get("_sha256"),
   748	                "canonical_source": r.get("owner_repository"),
   749	                "error": r.get("error"),
   750	            }
   751	            for r in registries
   752	        ],
   753	        "goals": evaluated,
   754	    }

    79	class ReceiptError(ValueError):
    80	    """A receipt file is malformed — recorded as a blocker, never fatal."""

    83	def _parse_iso(value: str) -> datetime:
    84	    return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    85	
    86	
    87	def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    88	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    89	    with path.open("rb") as handle:
    90	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(65536), b""):
    91	            digest.update(chunk)
    92	    return digest.hexdigest()
    93	
    94	
    95	def _hash_fd(fd: int) -> str:
    96	    """Hash the bytes of an ALREADY-OPEN descriptor, refusing anything
    97	    that is not a regular file (fstat on the open fd)."""
    98	    if not stat.S_ISREG(os.fstat(fd).st_mode):
    99	        raise ValueError(f"not a regular file: fd {fd}")
   100	    digest = hashlib.sha256()
   101	    with os.fdopen(fd, "rb", closefd=False) as handle:
   102	        for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(65536), b""):
   103	            digest.update(chunk)
   104	    return digest.hexdigest()
   105	
   106	
   107	def sha256_file_nofollow(path: Path) -> str:
   108	    """Hash a file WITHOUT a check-to-open window: one ``os.open`` with
   109	    ``O_NOFOLLOW`` so the final component cannot be swapped to a symlink
   110	    between the confinement check and the read (round-14 Sol finding 2),
   111	    and an ``fstat`` on the OPEN descriptor so the bytes hashed provably
   112	    belong to a regular file. Raises OSError (ELOOP etc.) on refusal and
   113	    ValueError when the opened object is not a regular file. Receipt-loop
   114	    artifacts use sha256_file_confined, which ALSO refuses symlinked
   115	    intermediate directories (round-15 Sol finding 1)."""
   116	    flags = os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
   117	    fd = os.open(path, flags)
   118	    try:
   119	        return _hash_fd(fd)
   120	    finally:
   121	        os.close(fd)
   122	
   123	
   124	def sha256_file_confined(root: Path, rel_parts) -> str:
   125	    """Hash a repo-relative file with NO symlink followed at ANY
   126	    component, closing the check-to-open window entirely: each directory
   127	    component is opened relative to the fd of its already-verified
   128	    parent (``dir_fd`` chaining) with ``O_DIRECTORY | O_NOFOLLOW``, and
   129	    the leaf with ``O_RDONLY | O_NOFOLLOW`` — so a concurrent writer who
   130	    swaps an INTERMEDIATE directory (or the final component) for a
   131	    symlink after the confinement check is refused with ELOOP at that
   132	    component's open (round-15 Sol finding 1); the pointed-at bytes are
   133	    never read. Raises OSError on refusal and ValueError when the opened
   134	    leaf is not a regular file."""
   135	    dir_flags = os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
   136	    leaf_flags = os.O_RDONLY | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
   137	    parts = [p for p in rel_parts if p not in (".", "")]
   138	    if not parts:
   139	        raise ValueError("empty relative path")
   140	    dir_fd = os.open(root, dir_flags)
   141	    try:
   142	        for part in parts[:-1]:
   143	            next_fd = os.open(part, dir_flags, dir_fd=dir_fd)
   144	            os.close(dir_fd)
   145	            dir_fd = next_fd
   146	        fd = os.open(parts[-1], leaf_flags, dir_fd=dir_fd)
   147	        try:
   148	            return _hash_fd(fd)
   149	        finally:
   150	            os.close(fd)
   151	    finally:
   152	        os.close(dir_fd)
   153	
   154	
   155	def sha256_bytes(data: bytes) -> str:
   156	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   157

   159	def load_policy(path: Path = DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   160	    policy = json.loads(json.dumps(DEFAULT_POLICY))
   161	    if path.exists():
   162	        try:
   163	            override = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   164	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   165	            return policy
   166	        for key in ("receipt_ttl_days", "independent_verifiers"):
   167	            if isinstance(override.get(key), dict):
   168	                policy[key].update(override[key])
   169	            elif isinstance(override.get(key), list):
   170	                policy[key] = override[key]
   171	        if isinstance(override.get("goal_ttl_overrides"), dict):
   172	            policy["goal_ttl_overrides"].update(override["goal_ttl_overrides"])
   173	    return policy

   176	def load_registries(directory: Path = DEFAULT_REGISTRIES_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   177	    registries = []
   178	    for path in sorted(directory.glob("finance-*-goals.json")):
   179	        try:
   180	            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   181	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   182	            registries.append(
   183	                {"file": path.name, "program_id": path.stem, "error": str(exc),
   184	                 "goals": []}
   185	            )
   186	            continue
   187	        data["_file"] = path.name
   188	        data["_sha256"] = sha256_file(path)
   189	        registries.append(data)
   190	    return registries
   191	
   192	
   193	def load_receipts(directory: Path = DEFAULT_RECEIPTS_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
   194	    receipts: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
   195	    if not directory.exists():
   196	        return receipts
   197	    for path in sorted(directory.glob("*.json")):
   198	        try:
   199	            data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
   200	        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   201	            receipts.append(
   202	                {"_file": path.name, "_invalid": f"unparseable: {exc}"}
   203	            )
   204	            continue
   205	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   206	            receipts.append({"_file": path.name, "_invalid": "not an object"})
   207	            continue
   208	        # NEVER trust a receipt-supplied _file: the real file name is the
   209	        # ledger key downstream, and a spoofed one could collide with (and
   210	        # silently overwrite) another receipt's bucket entry
   211	        data["_file"] = path.name
   212	        # ill-TYPED values poison the same way missing ones do: a list
   213	        # goal_id/kind/status would otherwise raise mid-derivation and
   214	        # take the whole /goals response down instead of locking
   215	        missing = [
   216	            f for f in REQUIRED_RECEIPT_FIELDS
   217	            if not isinstance(data.get(f), str) or not data.get(f, "").strip()
   218	        ]
   219	        problems: List[str] = []
   220	        if missing:
   221	            problems.append(f"missing/ill-typed fields: {missing}")
   222	        elif data["status"] not in VALID_STATUSES:
   223	            problems.append(
   224	                f"status {data['status']!r} not in {sorted(VALID_STATUSES)}"
   225	            )
   226	        artifacts = data.get("artifacts")
   227	        if artifacts is not None and (
   228	            not isinstance(artifacts, list)
   229	            or any(not isinstance(a, dict) for a in artifacts)
   230	        ):
   231	            problems.append("artifacts must be a list of objects")
   232	        if problems:
   233	            data["_invalid"] = "; ".join(problems)
   234	        receipts.append(data)
   235	    return receipts
   236	
   237	
   238	def _ttl_days(receipt: Dict[str, Any], policy: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[int]:
   239	    goal_overrides = policy.get("goal_ttl_overrides") or {}
   240	    per_goal = (goal_overrides.get(receipt.get("goal_id")) or {}).get(receipt["kind"])
   241	    if isinstance(per_goal, int):
   242	        return per_goal
   243	    return (policy.get("receipt_ttl_days") or {}).get(receipt["kind"])
   244	
   245	
   246	def _receipt_blockers(
   247	    receipt: Dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime, policy: Dict[str, Any],
   248	    repo_root: Path, check_artifacts: bool,
   249	) -> List[str]:
   250	    """Fail-closed wrapper: ANY unexpected failure while evaluating a
   251	    hostile receipt is itself a blocker (the receipt cannot vouch for
   252	    anything) — it can never take the whole /goals response down. This
   253	    is deliberate: reviews repeatedly found new ill-typed-input crashes,
   254	    so the invariant is enforced structurally, not per-field."""
   255	    try:
   256	        return _receipt_blockers_inner(
   257	            receipt, now=now, policy=policy, repo_root=repo_root,
   258	            check_artifacts=check_artifacts,
   259	        )
   260	    except Exception as exc:  # broad by design: fail closed, never 500
   261	        return [
   262	            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')!r}: could not be evaluated "
   263	            f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc}) — locked"
   264	        ]
   265	
   266	
   267	def _receipt_blockers_inner(
   268	    receipt: Dict[str, Any], *, now: datetime, policy: Dict[str, Any],
   269	    repo_root: Path, check_artifacts: bool,
   270	) -> List[str]:
   271	    if receipt.get("_invalid"):
   272	        return [f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: {receipt['_invalid']}"]
   273	    blockers: List[str] = []
   274	    try:
   275	        observed = _parse_iso(receipt["observed_at"])
   276	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   277	        return [f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: observed_at not ISO-8601"]
   278	    if observed.tzinfo is None:
   279	        observed = observed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   280	    ttl = _ttl_days(receipt, policy)
   281	    if ttl is None:
   282	        blockers.append(
   283	            f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: unknown kind {receipt['kind']!r}"
   284	        )
   285	    else:
   286	        try:
   287	            expiry = observed + timedelta(days=ttl)
   288	        except OverflowError:
   289	            # 9999-12-31T23:59:59Z + ttl overflows datetime; a receipt
   290	            # stamped at the far future edge is future-dated, not a crash
   291	            expiry = None
   292	        if expiry is not None and now > expiry:
   293	            blockers.append(
   294	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: stale (observed "
   295	                f"{receipt['observed_at']}, ttl {ttl}d)"
   296	            )
   297	        elif expiry is None:
   298	            blockers.append(
   299	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: future-dated (observed "
   300	                f"{receipt['observed_at']} beyond datetime range; recovers "
   301	                "only if re-stamped within range)"
   302	            )
   303	        elif observed > now + timedelta(minutes=FUTURE_SKEW_MINUTES):
   304	            # fail-closed but not permanent: the receipt ages into
   305	            # validity once the clock reaches its stamped time, so a
   306	            # single mis-dated receipt can never lock a goal forever
   307	            blockers.append(
   308	                f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: future-dated (observed "
   309	                f"{receipt['observed_at']}, now "
   310	                f"{now.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()}; recovers when "
   311	                "the clock reaches the stamped time)"
   312	            )
   313	    if check_artifacts:
   314	        for artifact in receipt.get("artifacts") or []:
   315	            if not isinstance(artifact, dict):
   316	                blockers.append(
   317	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: malformed artifact entry"
   318	                )
   319	                continue
   320	            label = artifact.get("label")
   321	            recorded = artifact.get("sha256")
   322	            art_path = artifact.get("path")
   323	            if (
   324	                not isinstance(label, str) or not label
   325	                or not isinstance(recorded, str) or not recorded
   326	                or not isinstance(art_path, str) or not art_path
   327	            ):
   328	                # ill-typed (list/object/int) fields are malformed, not a
   329	                # crash: they must lock the goal, not lose the response
   330	                blockers.append(
   331	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: malformed artifact entry"
   332	                )
   333	                continue
   334	            if art_path.startswith("repo:"):
   335	                # a repo:-prefixed artifact must stay INSIDE the repo:
   336	                # resolve and confine, so a receipt can never point the
   337	                # verifier at arbitrary host files (a symlink planted
   338	                # inside the repo that resolves outside is confined away
   339	                # too). Only the hash verdict would leak, but confinement
   340	                # is the fail-closed posture (GLM corroborating read,
   341	                # round-13 finding 4).
   342	                root = Path(repo_root).resolve()
   343	                path = (repo_root / art_path[5:]).resolve()
   344	                if not path.is_relative_to(root):
   345	                    blockers.append(
   346	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact path "
   347	                        f"escapes the repository: {art_path}"
   348	                    )
   349	                    continue
   350	                rel_parts = path.relative_to(root).parts
   351	            else:
   352	                path = Path(art_path)
   353	                rel_parts = None
   354	            if not path.exists():
   355	                blockers.append(
   356	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact missing: {art_path}"
   357	                )
   358	            elif not path.is_file():
   359	                # a well-typed path that names a DIRECTORY (or anything
   360	                # else non-regular) is a per-goal blocker, never an
   361	                # IsADirectoryError that loses the whole response
   362	                blockers.append(
   363	                    f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact is not a "
   364	                    f"regular file: {art_path}"
   365	                )
   366	            else:
   367	                try:
   368	                    # hash through the component-wise confined walker: the
   369	                    # confinement check above resolved this path, but a
   370	                    # concurrent writer could swap an intermediate
   371	                    # directory OR the final component for a symlink
   372	                    # before a plain open — the dir_fd-chained walk opens
   373	                    # every component with O_NOFOLLOW, so the check-to-
   374	                    # open window is closed, not merely narrowed
   375	                    # (round-14/15 Sol findings)
   376	                    if rel_parts is not None:
   377	                        actual = sha256_file_confined(root, rel_parts)
   378	                    else:
   379	                        actual = sha256_file_nofollow(path)
   380	                except OSError:
   381	                    blockers.append(
   382	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact "
   383	                        f"unreadable: {art_path}"
   384	                    )
   385	                except ValueError:
   386	                    blockers.append(
   387	                        f"receipt {receipt.get('_file')}: artifact "
   388	                        f"unreadable (not a regular file at open): "
   389	                        f"{art_path}"
   390	                    )
