    62	SPOT_PAIR_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Z0-9.]+/[A-Z0-9.]+$")
    63	TIMEFRAME_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+[smhd]$")
    64	TIMERANGE_RE = re.compile(r"^\d{8}(-\d{8})?(-)?$")
    65	STRATEGY_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$")
    66	CONFIG_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$")
    67	SHA256_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    68	
    69	# Extra freqtrade arguments are restricted to this allowlist. Freqtrade
    70	# merges multiple --config files with later files taking precedence and
    71	# lets --userdir/--strategy/--db-url override verified values, so those
    72	# (and anything unrecognised) are refused: they could re-introduce
    73	# credentials, derivatives settings or listeners the guard just rejected.
    74	ALLOWED_EXTRA_FLAGS = {
    75	    "--dl-trades", "--erase", "--prepend",
    76	    "--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days",
    77	}
    78	VALUE_EXTRA_FLAGS = {"--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days"}
    79	
    80	# The TYPED data channel (--pairs/--timeframe/--timerange -> data_args) is
    81	# validated by the same grammar the CLI applies: these flags, these value
    82	# shapes. data_args reaches docker only through build_docker_argv, which
    83	# enforces this allowlist itself — a caller forgetting CLI validation must
    84	# not be able to smuggle an unvalidated token into the argv.
    85	DATA_FLAGS_BOOLEAN = {"--dl-trades", "--erase", "--prepend"}
    86	DATA_FLAGS_VALUE = {"--timeframe", "--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days"}
    87	DATA_FLAGS_MULTI = {"--pairs"}
    88	DAYS_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+$")
    89	
    90	CONTAINER_NAME = "finance-ft00-dryrun"
    91	
    92	# Where the sha-bound entrypoint stages the verified config INSIDE the
    93	# container before freqtrade runs. Deliberately OUTSIDE the mounted
    94	# /freqtrade/user_data volume: the container's writable layer is not
    95	# reachable by any host-side process that can merely write the mounted
    96	# workspace, so the checksum and freqtrade's open() act on bytes no
    97	# workspace-writer can replace.
    98	PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH = "/tmp/ft-verified-config.json"
    99	
   100	# Exchanges usable with PUBLIC data only (no key required for OHLCV).
   112	
   113	
   114	def validate_extra_args(extra_args: Optional[List[str]]) -> List[str]:
   115	    """Findings for extra freqtrade tokens outside the safe allowlist.
   116	    Empty list == safe. Freqtrade overlays later ``--config`` files onto
   117	    earlier ones, so an unchecked extra ``--config`` could re-introduce
   118	    credentials, futures settings or an enabled API server after the
   119	    main config passed the guard."""
   120	    findings: List[str] = []
   121	    tokens = list(extra_args or [])
   122	    i = 0
   123	    while i < len(tokens):
   124	        token = tokens[i]
   125	        if token not in ALLOWED_EXTRA_FLAGS:
   126	            findings.append(
   127	                f"extra freqtrade argument {token!r} is not in the safe "
   128	                f"allowlist {sorted(ALLOWED_EXTRA_FLAGS)} (overlay flags "
   129	                "such as --config/-c/--userdir/--strategy are never "
   130	                "permitted)"
   131	            )
   132	            i += 1
   133	            continue
   134	        if token in VALUE_EXTRA_FLAGS:
   135	            if i + 1 >= len(tokens):
   136	                findings.append(f"extra argument {token!r} requires a value")
   137	            elif tokens[i + 1].startswith("-"):
   138	                # a flag-looking token is never accepted as a value: it
   139	                # would smuggle a disallowed flag past the allowlist
   140	                findings.append(
   141	                    f"extra argument {token!r} requires a non-flag value "
   142	                    f"(got {tokens[i + 1]!r})"
   143	                )
   144	            else:
   145	                i += 1  # consume the flag's value
   146	        i += 1
   147	    return findings
   148	
   149	
   150	def validate_data_args(data_args: Optional[List[str]]) -> List[str]:
   151	    """Findings for typed download tokens outside the safe grammar.
   152	    Empty list == safe. Mirrors the CLI's typed validation so
   153	    build_docker_argv enforces the same shapes itself."""
   154	    findings: List[str] = []
   155	    tokens = list(data_args or [])
   156	    value_checkers: Dict[str, Any] = {
   157	        "--timeframe": (TIMEFRAME_RE, "<n><s|m|h|d>"),
   158	        "--timerange": (TIMERANGE_RE, "YYYYMMDD[-YYYYMMDD][-]"),
   159	        "--days": (DAYS_RE, "a positive integer"),
   160	        "--new-pairs-days": (DAYS_RE, "a positive integer"),
   161	    }
   162	    i = 0
   163	    while i < len(tokens):
   164	        token = tokens[i]
   165	        if token in DATA_FLAGS_BOOLEAN:
   166	            i += 1
   167	            continue
   168	        if token in value_checkers:
   169	            pattern, shape = value_checkers[token]
   170	            if i + 1 >= len(tokens):
   171	                findings.append(f"data argument {token!r} requires a value")
   172	            elif tokens[i + 1].startswith("-"):
   173	                findings.append(
   174	                    f"data argument {token!r} requires a non-flag value "
   175	                    f"(got {tokens[i + 1]!r})"
   176	                )
   177	            elif not pattern.match(tokens[i + 1]):
   178	                findings.append(
   179	                    f"data argument {token!r} value {tokens[i + 1]!r} is "
   180	                    f"not {shape}"
   181	                )
   182	            i += 2
   183	            continue
   184	        if token == "--pairs":
   185	            pairs: List[str] = []
   186	            j = i + 1
   187	            while j < len(tokens) and not tokens[j].startswith("-"):
   188	                pairs.append(tokens[j])
   189	                j += 1
   190	            if not pairs:
   191	                findings.append("data argument '--pairs' requires >=1 pair")
   192	            for pair in pairs:
   193	                if not SPOT_PAIR_RE.match(pair):
   194	                    findings.append(
   195	                        f"--pairs entry {pair!r} is not a plain spot pair "
   196	                        "(no ':' derivatives)"
   197	                    )
   198	            i = j
   199	            continue
   200	        findings.append(
   201	            f"data argument {token!r} is not in the typed download grammar "
   202	            f"(boolean {sorted(DATA_FLAGS_BOOLEAN)}, value "
   203	            f"{sorted(DATA_FLAGS_VALUE)}, multi {sorted(DATA_FLAGS_MULTI)}); "
   204	            "raw tokens belong to the --extra allowlist channel"
   205	        )
   206	        i += 1
   207	    return findings
   208	
   209	
   210	def verify_config(cfg: Dict[str, Any], image_ref: str) -> List[str]:
   211	    """Return a list of safety findings. Empty list == safe to run."""
   212	    findings: List[str] = []
   213	
   214	    if image_ref != DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE:
   215	        findings.append(
   216	            f"engine image {image_ref!r} is not the pinned official "
   217	            f"reference — only the exact digest-pinned official image "
   218	            f"{DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE!r} may run"
   219	        )
   220	
   221	    if cfg.get("add_config_files"):
   222	        findings.append(
   223	            "add_config_files is present — overlay config files bypass "
   224	            "the verified configuration and are not permitted"
   225	        )
   226	
   227	    if cfg.get("dry_run") is not True:
   228	        findings.append("dry_run is not strictly true")
   229	
   230	    mode = cfg.get("trading_mode")
   231	    if mode is not None and mode != "spot":
   232	        findings.append(f"trading_mode is {mode!r}, only spot is permitted")
   233	    if cfg.get("margin_enabled"):
   234	        findings.append("margin_enabled is true")
   235	    if cfg.get("liquidation_buffer") is not None:
   236	        findings.append("liquidation_buffer present (derivatives surface)")
   237	
   238	    exchange = cfg.get("exchange") or {}
   239	    name = (exchange.get("name") or "").lower()
   240	    if name not in ALLOWED_PUBLIC_SPOT_EXCHANGES:
   241	        findings.append(
   242	            f"exchange.name {name!r} not in public-data spot allowlist "
   243	            f"{sorted(ALLOWED_PUBLIC_SPOT_EXCHANGES)}"
   244	        )
   245	    for cred_field in ("key", "secret", "uid", "password"):
   246	        if exchange.get(cred_field):
   247	            findings.append(
   248	                f"exchange.{cred_field} is set — no exchange credentials are "
   249	                "permitted in simulation-only mode"
   250	            )
   251	
   252	    whitelist = exchange.get("pair_whitelist") or []
   253	    if not whitelist:
   254	        findings.append("pair_whitelist is empty")
   255	    for pair in whitelist:
   256	        if not isinstance(pair, str) or not SPOT_PAIR_RE.match(pair):
   257	            findings.append(f"pair {pair!r} is not a plain spot pair (no ':' derivatives)")
   258	
   259	    api_server = cfg.get("api_server")
   260	    if api_server:
   261	        if api_server.get("enabled"):
   262	            findings.append("api_server.enabled is true")
   263	        listen = (api_server.get("listen_ip_address") or "")
   264	        if listen and listen not in ("127.0.0.1", "localhost"):
   265	            findings.append(
   266	                f"api_server.listen_ip_address {listen!r} is not loopback"
   267	            )
   268	
   269	    telegram = cfg.get("telegram")
   270	    if telegram and telegram.get("enabled"):
   271	        findings.append("telegram.enabled is true")
   272	
   273	    strategy = cfg.get("strategy")
   274	    if not isinstance(strategy, str) or not STRATEGY_RE.match(strategy):
   275	        findings.append(f"strategy {strategy!r} is not a plain identifier")
   276	
   277	    return findings
   278	
   279	
   280	def load_config(path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   284	def build_docker_argv(
   285	    action: str,
   286	    *,
   287	    image_ref: str,
   288	    user_data_dir: Path,
   289	    config_name: str = "config-ft00.json",
   290	    strategy: Optional[str] = None,
   291	    extra_args: Optional[List[str]] = None,
   292	    data_args: Optional[List[str]] = None,
   293	    name: str = CONTAINER_NAME,
   294	    detach: bool = False,
   295	    uid_gid: Optional[str] = None,
   296	    config_sha256: Optional[str] = None,
   297	) -> List[str]:
   298	    """Construct the docker command for a supported action. The pinned
   299	    image is embedded verbatim; the user_data workspace is the only
   300	    volume; no network publication is ever added.
   301	
   302	    ``data_args`` carries FIRST-CLASS typed download arguments (pairs /
   303	    timeframe / timerange) and must pass :func:`validate_data_args` —
   304	    the builder does not trust the caller to have validated them;
   305	    ``extra_args`` is the raw user-supplied remainder and must pass
   306	    :func:`validate_extra_args`.
   307	
   308	    When ``config_sha256`` (a full sha256 of the snapshot's bytes) is
   309	    given for a config-executing action, the container entrypoint
   310	    becomes ``/bin/sh -c '<copy> && <checksum check> && exec "$@"'``:
   311	    the mounted snapshot is FIRST copied to PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH (the
   312	    container's own writable layer — never inside the mounted volume, so
   313	    no host-side process can touch it), the COPY's sha256 is verified,
   314	    and only then does freqtrade exec — reading the copy. This closes
   315	    the check-to-use race: the file freqtrade opens is never a path a
   316	    workspace-writer can replace, because check and use are the same
   317	    inode in container-private storage.
   318	
   319	    By default the container runs with the image's own lifecycle (root →
   320	    entrypoint chown of user_data to its internal ftuser → drop to
   321	    ftuser): freqtrade is pip-installed under ftuser's 0700 home, so an
   322	    explicit ``--user`` cannot reach the binary. Pass ``uid_gid``
   323	    explicitly only with an image that supports it."""
   324	    if image_ref != DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE or not DIGEST_RE.match(image_ref):
   325	        raise ValueError(
   326	            "image_ref must be exactly the pinned official reference "
   327	            f"{DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE!r}: got {image_ref!r}"
   328	        )
   329	    overlay = validate_extra_args(extra_args)
   330	    if overlay:
   331	        raise ValueError("; ".join(overlay))
   332	    data_findings = validate_data_args(data_args)
   333	    if data_findings:
   334	        raise ValueError("; ".join(data_findings))
   335	    if strategy is not None and not STRATEGY_RE.match(strategy):
   336	        # strategy is config-controlled text; it reaches docker argv and
   337	        # (in the hash-bound form) a shell script — only identifiers pass
   338	        raise ValueError(f"strategy {strategy!r} is not a plain identifier")
   339	    if not CONFIG_NAME_RE.match(config_name):
   340	        raise ValueError(f"config_name {config_name!r} has unsafe characters")
   341	
   342	    volume = f"{user_data_dir.resolve()}:/freqtrade/user_data"
   343	    config_arg = f"/freqtrade/user_data/{config_name}"
   344	    # In the sha-bound form freqtrade must read the CONTAINER-PRIVATE
   345	    # copy, never the mounted path (see PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH). The
   346	    # builder refuses a private path that would fall inside the mount.
   347	    if config_sha256 is not None and action in ("download-data", "dry-run"):
   348	        if PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH.startswith("/freqtrade/user_data/"):
   349	            raise ValueError(
   350	                "PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH must live outside the mounted volume"
   351	            )
   352	        exec_config_arg = PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH
   353	    else:
   354	        exec_config_arg = config_arg
   355	    cmd_args: List[str]
   356	    if action == "version":
   357	        cmd_args = ["--version"]
   358	    elif action == "download-data":
   359	        cmd_args = [
   360	            "download-data", "--config", exec_config_arg,
   361	            "--userdir", "/freqtrade/user_data",
   362	        ] + list(data_args or []) + list(extra_args or [])
   363	    elif action == "dry-run":
   364	        cmd_args = [
   365	            "trade", "--config", exec_config_arg,
   366	            "--userdir", "/freqtrade/user_data",
   367	            "--dry-run", "--strategy", strategy or "",
   368	        ] + list(extra_args or [])
   369	    else:
   370	        raise ValueError(f"unknown action {action!r}")
   371	
   372	    # Foreground commands self-clean with --rm; a detached smoke run must
   373	    # NOT use --rm, so a crash leaves the container's logs inspectable
   374	    # until we remove it explicitly after capturing evidence.
   375	    argv = ["docker", "run"]
   376	    if not detach:
   377	        argv.append("--rm")
   378	    argv += ["--name", name, "--stop-timeout", "60", "-v", volume]
   379	    if uid_gid:
   380	        argv += ["--user", uid_gid]
   381	    if detach:
   382	        argv.append("-d")
   383	
   384	    if config_sha256 is not None:
   385	        # Bind the config bytes INSIDE the container, at open time: the
   386	        # entrypoint COPIES the mounted snapshot to PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH
   387	        # (container-private storage — a host-side workspace writer
   388	        # cannot replace what it cannot address), verifies the COPY's
   389	        # sha256, and only then execs freqtrade, which reads the copy.
   390	        # The checksum and freqtrade's open() therefore act on the same
   391	        # unreachable-from-the-host inode: there is no window between
   392	        # check and use. Freqtrade args travel as positional parameters
   393	        # ("$@"), never through the shell string, so no token of them
   394	        # can inject.
   395	        if action not in ("download-data", "dry-run"):
   396	            raise ValueError(
   397	                "config_sha256 binding applies only to config-executing "
   398	                "actions"
   399	            )
   400	        if not SHA256_RE.match(config_sha256):
   401	            raise ValueError("config_sha256 must be a full sha256 hex digest")
   402	        script = (
   403	            f"cp {config_arg} {PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH} && "
   404	            f"printf '%s  %s\\n' {config_sha256} {PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH} "
   405	            '| sha256sum -c --status - && exec "$@"'
   406	        )
   407	        # "$@" expands to the params AFTER argv0, so the full command —
   408	        # the freqtrade binary AND its subcommand — must be positional
   409	        # params; argv0 itself is never executed
   410	        argv += ["--entrypoint", "/bin/sh", image_ref, "-c", script,
   411	                 "sh", "freqtrade"] + cmd_args
   412	    else:
   413	        argv.append(image_ref)
   414	        argv += cmd_args
   580	    for field_name in ("key", "secret", "uid", "password"):
   581	        if ex.get(field_name):
   582	            ex[field_name] = "<redacted>"
   583	    return safe
   584	
   585	
   586	def build_snapshot_write_argv(user_data_dir: Path, name: str) -> List[str]:
   587	    """docker argv writing the verified-config snapshot via the pinned
   588	    image (bytes on stdin -> file). Needed because a running freqtrade
   589	    container chowns the mounted user_data tree to its internal user,
   590	    locking the invoking account out of direct writes.
   591	
   592	    ``set -C`` (noclobber) makes the redirect open with O_CREAT|O_EXCL
   593	    semantics: if ANYTHING appears at the path between the runner's
   594	    clear and the container's open — in particular a replanted SYMLINK
   595	    — the open fails instead of following the link and truncating its
   596	    target. A refused write fails the snapshot (fail closed); a
   597	    truncated innocent host file can never be undone."""
   598	    return [
   599	        "docker", "run", "--rm", "-i",
   600	        "--entrypoint", "/bin/sh",
   601	        "-v", f"{user_data_dir.resolve()}:/freqtrade/user_data",
   602	        DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE,
   603	        "-c", f"set -C; cat > /freqtrade/user_data/{name}",
   604	    ]
   605	
   606	
   607	def build_snapshot_rm_argv(user_data_dir: Path, name: str) -> List[str]:
   608	    """docker argv removing a snapshot file via the pinned image
   609	    (best-effort cleanup under the same uid-1000 constraint)."""
   610	    return [
   611	        "docker", "run", "--rm",
   612	        "--entrypoint", "/bin/rm",
   613	        "-v", f"{user_data_dir.resolve()}:/freqtrade/user_data",
   614	        DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE,
   615	        "-f", f"/freqtrade/user_data/{name}",
   616	    ]
   617	
   618	
   619	def _assert_snapshot_regular(target: Path) -> None:
   620	    """The snapshot must be a REGULAR file, not a symlink: docker would
   621	    otherwise follow the link at container start and execute whatever it
   622	    points at — e.g. the still-mutable original config."""
   623	    st = os.lstat(target)
   624	    if stat.S_ISLNK(st.st_mode) or not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   625	        raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   626	            f"verified-config snapshot {target.name} is not a regular "
   627	            "file (symlink?) — refusing to execute it"
   628	        )
   629	
   630	
   631	def _assert_snapshot_fresh(target: Path, original: Path) -> None:
   632	    """The snapshot must be an inode the runner itself just created: a
   633	    HARDLINK to the original config passes the regular-file and byte
   634	    checks while sharing its inode, so an in-place edit of the original
   635	    would silently change the "snapshot" too. nlink must be exactly 1
   636	    and the inode must differ from the original's."""
   637	    st = os.lstat(target)
   638	    if st.st_nlink != 1:
   639	        raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   640	            f"verified-config snapshot {target.name} has {st.st_nlink} "
   641	            "hard links — refusing to execute it"
   642	        )
   643	    try:
   644	        ost = os.lstat(original)
   645	    except OSError:
   646	        return
   647	    if (st.st_dev, st.st_ino) == (ost.st_dev, ost.st_ino):
   648	        raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   649	            f"verified-config snapshot {target.name} aliases the "
   650	            "original config file (same inode) — refusing to execute it"
   651	        )
   652	
   653	
   654	def materialise_verified_config(
   655	    cfg_path: Path, user_data: Path, cfg: Dict[str, Any],
   656	    verified_raw: bytes,
   657	) -> Path:
   658	    """Snapshot the VERIFIED config bytes to a content-addressed file
   659	    directly inside the workspace and return its path.
   660	
   661	    Closes the verify→execute TOCTOU window: the guard verifies content
   662	    loaded from ``cfg_path``, but docker later loads whatever bytes are
   663	    at that path when the container starts. ``verified_raw`` MUST be the
   664	    exact bytes of the single read the guard parsed — captured by the
   665	    caller at verification time — and the file's CURRENT bytes must
   666	    still equal them (byte comparison; parsed equality would let JSON
   667	    ``true`` stand in for ``1``).
   668	
   669	    The snapshot is ALWAYS a FRESH inode: a pre-existing entry at the
   670	    content-addressed path (hardlink to the mutable original, symlink,
   671	    stale bytes) is removed first, then the file is created with
   672	    O_CREAT|O_EXCL (and O_NOFOLLOW where available). Afterwards the
   673	    snapshot must be a regular file with nlink == 1 whose inode differs
   674	    from the original's, and whose bytes re-read from the host equal the
   675	    verified bytes exactly. Raises ConfigUnsafeError on any mismatch
   676	    (fail closed).
   677	
   678	    When the invoking user cannot write the workspace directly (the
   679	    freqtrade container re-chowns it to its internal uid on every run),
   680	    the write is delegated to a one-shot pinned-image container; the
   681	    inode/nlink/byte assertions still run against the host file."""
   682	    raw = Path(cfg_path).read_bytes()
   683	    if raw != verified_raw:
   684	        raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   685	            "the config file changed between guard verification and "
   686	            "snapshot materialisation — refusing to execute it"
   687	        )
   688	    digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()[:12]
   689	    target = Path(user_data) / f".verified-{digest}.json"
   690	    if os.path.lexists(target):
   691	        # never accept a pre-existing inode (a hardlink to the original
   692	        # passes lstat + byte checks while aliasing a mutable file);
   693	        # clear it and create fresh below
   694	        remove_snapshot_best_effort(Path(user_data), target.name)
   695	        if os.path.lexists(target):
   696	            raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   697	                f"verified-config snapshot path {target.name} exists and "
   698	                "could not be cleared (symlink/hardlink/stale file) — "
   699	                "refusing to write"
   700	            )
   701	    nofollow = getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
   702	    try:
   703	        fd = os.open(
   704	            target,
   705	            os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | nofollow, 0o644,
   706	        )
   707	        with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as handle:
   708	            handle.write(raw)
   709	    except PermissionError:
   710	        proc = subprocess.run(
   711	            build_snapshot_write_argv(Path(user_data), target.name),
   712	            input=raw, capture_output=True, timeout=120,
   713	        )
   714	        if proc.returncode != 0:
   715	            raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   716	                "verified-config snapshot could not be written (direct "
   717	                "write not permitted and pinned-image delegation failed): "
   718	                + proc.stderr.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")[-300:]
   719	            )
   720	    except FileExistsError:
   721	        raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   722	            f"verified-config snapshot path {target.name} appeared "
   723	            "between the clear and the create — refusing to write"
   724	        ) from None
   725	    try:
   726	        _assert_snapshot_regular(target)
   727	        _assert_snapshot_fresh(target, Path(cfg_path))
   728	        reread = target.read_bytes()
   729	        if reread != raw:
   730	            # byte-exact: not parsed-object equality (which would accept
   731	            # true↔1 swaps) and not a truncated-hash comparison
   732	            raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   733	                f"verified-config round-trip mismatch for {target}: "
   734	                "snapshotted bytes differ from the verified bytes"
   735	            )
   736	        reparsed = json.loads(raw.decode("utf-8"))
   737	        if reparsed != cfg:
   738	            raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   739	                "verified bytes do not parse as the config the guard "
   740	                "verified — refusing to execute"
   741	            )
   742	    except Exception:
   788	    cfg_path = Path(args.config)
   789	    try:
   790	        # ONE read: the parsed config and its exact bytes must come from
   791	        # the same read, so the verified-bytes binding enforced later by
   792	        # materialise_verified_config is sound (a second read would
   793	        # reopen a swap window between parse and capture)
   794	        verified_raw = cfg_path.read_bytes()
   795	        cfg = json.loads(verified_raw.decode("utf-8"))
   796	    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
   797	        print(json.dumps({"ok": False, "error": f"config unreadable: {exc}"}, indent=2))
   798	        return 2
   799	
   800	    if args.action == "show-config":
   801	        print(json.dumps(redact_config(cfg), indent=2))
   802	        return 0
   803	
   804	    findings = verify_config(cfg, args.image)
   805	    findings += validate_extra_args(args.extra)
   806	
   807	    # The VERIFIED config and the EXECUTED config must be the same file:
   808	    # docker loads the config from the mounted user_data workspace, so a
   809	    # --config anywhere else would let a safe file pass the guard while
   810	    # an unsafe one runs.
   811	    user_data = Path(args.user_data_dir)
   812	    try:
   813	        config_in_workspace = (
   814	            cfg_path.resolve().parent == user_data.resolve()
   815	        )
   816	    except OSError:
   817	        config_in_workspace = False
   818	    if not config_in_workspace:
   819	        findings.append(
   820	            "--config must point at a file directly inside "
   821	            f"--user-data-dir (verified config and executed config must "
   822	            f"be the same file): {cfg_path} vs {user_data}"
   823	        )
   824	
   825	    # First-class download arguments are typed and validated here; they
   826	    # travel via data_args (never the raw --extra channel).
   827	    data_args: List[str] = []
   828	    for pair in args.pairs:
   829	        if not SPOT_PAIR_RE.match(pair):
   830	            findings.append(f"--pairs entry {pair!r} is not a plain spot pair")
   831	    if args.pairs:
   832	        data_args += ["--pairs"] + list(args.pairs)
   833	    if args.timeframe:
   834	        if not TIMEFRAME_RE.match(args.timeframe):
   835	            findings.append(
   836	                f"--timeframe {args.timeframe!r} is not <n><s|m|h|d>"
   837	            )
   838	        data_args += ["--timeframe", args.timeframe]
   839	    if args.timerange:
   840	        if not TIMERANGE_RE.match(args.timerange):
   841	            findings.append(
   842	                f"--timerange {args.timerange!r} is not YYYYMMDD[-YYYYMMDD][-]"
   843	            )
   844	        data_args += ["--timerange", args.timerange]
   845	
   846	    if findings:
   847	        print(json.dumps(
   848	            {
   849	                "ok": False,
   850	                "action": args.action,
   851	                "fail_closed": True,
   852	                "findings": findings,
   853	            }, indent=2))
   854	        return 3
   855	
   856	    strategy = cfg.get("strategy")
   857	    config_name = cfg_path.name
   858	    log_dir = Path(args.log_dir)
   859	
   860	    if args.action == "verify-config":
   861	        print(json.dumps(
   862	            {
   863	                "ok": True, "fail_closed": True, "findings": [],
   864	                "image": args.image, "strategy": strategy,
   865	                "dry_run": cfg.get("dry_run"),
   866	                "trading_mode": cfg.get("trading_mode") or "spot(default)",
   867	            }, indent=2))
   868	        return 0
   869	
   870	    if args.action == "version":
   871	        result = run_command(
   872	            build_docker_argv("version", image_ref=args.image,
   873	                              user_data_dir=user_data,
   874	                              config_name=config_name),
   875	            timeout_s=120, log_path=log_dir / "version.log",
   876	        )
   877	        print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
   878	        return 0 if result["returncode"] == 0 else 4
   879	
   880	    # Actions that EXECUTE the config close the verify→execute TOCTOU
   881	    # window first: docker loads a content-addressed snapshot of the
   882	    # verified bytes, not the mutable original path.
   883	    try:
   884	        verified_path = materialise_verified_config(
   885	            cfg_path, user_data, cfg, verified_raw)
   886	    except (OSError, ConfigUnsafeError) as exc:
   887	        print(json.dumps(
   888	            {
   889	                "ok": False, "action": args.action, "fail_closed": True,
   890	                "findings": [f"verified config could not be snapshotted: {exc}"],
   891	            }, indent=2))
   892	        return 3
   893	    try:
   894	        config_name = verified_path.name
   895	        try:
   896	            # re-assert immediately before handing the path to docker: the
   897	            # snapshot must still be a regular file whose bytes hash to
   898	            # the digest encoded in its content-addressed name
   899	            _assert_snapshot_regular(verified_path)
   900	            snap_digest = hashlib.sha256(
   901	                verified_path.read_bytes()
   902	            ).hexdigest()[:12]
   903	            if snap_digest != config_name[len(".verified-"):-len(".json")]:
   904	                raise ConfigUnsafeError(
   905	                    "verified-config snapshot no longer matches its "

   851	def build_report(
   852	    collected_at: str,
   853	    results: List[SourceResult],
   854	    market_context: Dict[str, Any],
   855	    previous: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
   856	    params: Dict[str, Any],
   857	) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   858	    records = [r for res in results for r in res.records]
   859	    topics = aggregate_topics(records)
   860	    prev_report = (previous or {}).get("report") or previous or {}
   861	    baseline_note = (
   862	        "no prior snapshot (first collection); baseline comparison unavailable"
   863	        if not previous
   864	        else f"baseline snapshot {previous.get('generated_at')} "
   865	        f"({len(prev_report.get('topics') or [])} topics)"
   866	    )
   867	    return {
   868	        "probe": PROBE_NAME,
   869	        "probe_version": PROBE_VERSION,
   870	        "generated_at": collected_at,
   871	        "params": params,
   872	        "source_results": [
   873	            {
   874	                "source": res.source,
   875	                "status": res.status,
   876	                "records": len(res.records),
   877	                "http_status": res.http_status,
   878	                "retries": res.retries,
   879	                "deduped_exact": res.deduped_exact,
   880	                "deduped_syndication": res.deduped_syndication,
   881	                "error": res.error,
   882	            }
   883	            for res in results
   884	        ],
   885	        "topics": topics,
   886	        "market_context": market_context,
   887	        "baseline": baseline_note,
   888	        "comparators": (
   889	            "source diversity = distinct registrable domains; syndicated "
   890	            "duplicates deduplicated; naive keyword alert = single-source count"
   891	        ),
   892	        "excluded_sources": [
   893	            "x-cookies", "private-reddit", "youtube-internal",
   894	            "retailer-scraping", "paid-apis",
   895	        ],
   896	    }

    50	# Official image pinned by digest (tag ``stable`` resolved 2026-10-29,
    51	# image created 2026-09-29, Python 3.14.7 / CCXT 4.5.84). The guard
    52	# requires this reference EXACTLY: a digest alone proves nothing about
    53	# which repository it names, so ``evil/freqtrade@sha256:<same digest>``
    54	# must not pass, and a bare tag is never accepted — pinning must be by
    55	# digest so a re-pulled "stable" cannot silently change the engine under
    56	# an attestation.
    57	DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE = (
    58	    "freqtradeorg/freqtrade@sha256:"
    59	    "9d67afb8eb5f4e1210f4fb067efcbd5e005bd4f1c27f6f3888b8b9fc7f9b5199"
    60	)
    61	DIGEST_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9./_-]+@sha256:[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    62	SPOT_PAIR_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Z0-9.]+/[A-Z0-9.]+$")
    63	TIMEFRAME_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+[smhd]$")
    64	TIMERANGE_RE = re.compile(r"^\d{8}(-\d{8})?(-)?$")
    65	STRATEGY_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$")
    66	CONFIG_NAME_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9._-]+$")
    67	SHA256_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
    68	
    69	# Extra freqtrade arguments are restricted to this allowlist. Freqtrade
    70	# merges multiple --config files with later files taking precedence and
    71	# lets --userdir/--strategy/--db-url override verified values, so those
    72	# (and anything unrecognised) are refused: they could re-introduce
    73	# credentials, derivatives settings or listeners the guard just rejected.
    74	ALLOWED_EXTRA_FLAGS = {
    75	    "--dl-trades", "--erase", "--prepend",
    76	    "--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days",
    77	}
    78	VALUE_EXTRA_FLAGS = {"--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days"}
    79	
    80	# The TYPED data channel (--pairs/--timeframe/--timerange -> data_args) is
    81	# validated by the same grammar the CLI applies: these flags, these value
    82	# shapes. data_args reaches docker only through build_docker_argv, which
    83	# enforces this allowlist itself — a caller forgetting CLI validation must
    84	# not be able to smuggle an unvalidated token into the argv.
    85	DATA_FLAGS_BOOLEAN = {"--dl-trades", "--erase", "--prepend"}
    86	DATA_FLAGS_VALUE = {"--timeframe", "--timerange", "--days", "--new-pairs-days"}
    87	DATA_FLAGS_MULTI = {"--pairs"}
    88	DAYS_RE = re.compile(r"^\d+$")
    89

    92	# Where the sha-bound entrypoint stages the verified config INSIDE the
    93	# container before freqtrade runs. Deliberately OUTSIDE the mounted
   112

   114	def validate_extra_args(extra_args: Optional[List[str]]) -> List[str]:
   115	    """Findings for extra freqtrade tokens outside the safe allowlist.
   116	    Empty list == safe. Freqtrade overlays later ``--config`` files onto
   117	    earlier ones, so an unchecked extra ``--config`` could re-introduce
   118	    credentials, futures settings or an enabled API server after the
   119	    main config passed the guard."""
   120	    findings: List[str] = []
   121	    tokens = list(extra_args or [])
   122	    i = 0
   123	    while i < len(tokens):
   124	        token = tokens[i]
   125	        if token not in ALLOWED_EXTRA_FLAGS:
   126	            findings.append(
   127	                f"extra freqtrade argument {token!r} is not in the safe "
   128	                f"allowlist {sorted(ALLOWED_EXTRA_FLAGS)} (overlay flags "
   129	                "such as --config/-c/--userdir/--strategy are never "
   130	                "permitted)"
   131	            )
   132	            i += 1
   133	            continue
   134	        if token in VALUE_EXTRA_FLAGS:
   135	            if i + 1 >= len(tokens):
   136	                findings.append(f"extra argument {token!r} requires a value")
   137	            elif tokens[i + 1].startswith("-"):
   138	                # a flag-looking token is never accepted as a value: it
   139	                # would smuggle a disallowed flag past the allowlist
   140	                findings.append(
   141	                    f"extra argument {token!r} requires a non-flag value "
   142	                    f"(got {tokens[i + 1]!r})"
   143	                )
   144	            else:
   145	                i += 1  # consume the flag's value
   146	        i += 1
   147	    return findings
   148	
   149

   210	def verify_config(cfg: Dict[str, Any], image_ref: str) -> List[str]:
   211	    """Return a list of safety findings. Empty list == safe to run."""
   212	    findings: List[str] = []
   213	
   214	    if image_ref != DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE:
   215	        findings.append(
   216	            f"engine image {image_ref!r} is not the pinned official "
   217	            f"reference — only the exact digest-pinned official image "
   218	            f"{DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE!r} may run"
   219	        )
   220	
   221	    if cfg.get("add_config_files"):
   222	        findings.append(
   223	            "add_config_files is present — overlay config files bypass "
   224	            "the verified configuration and are not permitted"
   225	        )
   226	
   227	    if cfg.get("dry_run") is not True:
   228	        findings.append("dry_run is not strictly true")
   229	
   230	    mode = cfg.get("trading_mode")
   231	    if mode is not None and mode != "spot":
   232	        findings.append(f"trading_mode is {mode!r}, only spot is permitted")
   233	    if cfg.get("margin_enabled"):
   234	        findings.append("margin_enabled is true")
   235	    if cfg.get("liquidation_buffer") is not None:
   236	        findings.append("liquidation_buffer present (derivatives surface)")
   237	
   238	    exchange = cfg.get("exchange") or {}
   239	    name = (exchange.get("name") or "").lower()
   240	    if name not in ALLOWED_PUBLIC_SPOT_EXCHANGES:
   241	        findings.append(
   242	            f"exchange.name {name!r} not in public-data spot allowlist "
   243	            f"{sorted(ALLOWED_PUBLIC_SPOT_EXCHANGES)}"
   244	        )
   245	    for cred_field in ("key", "secret", "uid", "password"):
   246	        if exchange.get(cred_field):
   247	            findings.append(
   248	                f"exchange.{cred_field} is set — no exchange credentials are "
   249	                "permitted in simulation-only mode"
   250	            )
   251	
   252	    whitelist = exchange.get("pair_whitelist") or []
   253	    if not whitelist:
   254	        findings.append("pair_whitelist is empty")
   255	    for pair in whitelist:
   256	        if not isinstance(pair, str) or not SPOT_PAIR_RE.match(pair):
   257	            findings.append(f"pair {pair!r} is not a plain spot pair (no ':' derivatives)")
   258	
   259	    api_server = cfg.get("api_server")
   260	    if api_server:
   261	        if api_server.get("enabled"):
   262	            findings.append("api_server.enabled is true")
   263	        listen = (api_server.get("listen_ip_address") or "")
   264	        if listen and listen not in ("127.0.0.1", "localhost"):
   265	            findings.append(
   266	                f"api_server.listen_ip_address {listen!r} is not loopback"
   267	            )
   268	
   269	    telegram = cfg.get("telegram")
   270	    if telegram and telegram.get("enabled"):
   271	        findings.append("telegram.enabled is true")
   272	
   273	    strategy = cfg.get("strategy")
   274	    if not isinstance(strategy, str) or not STRATEGY_RE.match(strategy):
   275	        findings.append(f"strategy {strategy!r} is not a plain identifier")
   276	
   277	    return findings
   278	
   279	
   280	def load_config(path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
   284	def build_docker_argv(
   285	    action: str,
   286	    *,
   287	    image_ref: str,
   288	    user_data_dir: Path,
   289	    config_name: str = "config-ft00.json",
   290	    strategy: Optional[str] = None,
   291	    extra_args: Optional[List[str]] = None,
   292	    data_args: Optional[List[str]] = None,
   293	    name: str = CONTAINER_NAME,
   294	    detach: bool = False,
   295	    uid_gid: Optional[str] = None,
   296	    config_sha256: Optional[str] = None,
   297	) -> List[str]:
   298	    """Construct the docker command for a supported action. The pinned
   299	    image is embedded verbatim; the user_data workspace is the only
   300	    volume; no network publication is ever added.
   301	
   302	    ``data_args`` carries FIRST-CLASS typed download arguments (pairs /
   303	    timeframe / timerange) and must pass :func:`validate_data_args` —
   304	    the builder does not trust the caller to have validated them;
   305	    ``extra_args`` is the raw user-supplied remainder and must pass
   306	    :func:`validate_extra_args`.
   307	
   308	    When ``config_sha256`` (a full sha256 of the snapshot's bytes) is
   309	    given for a config-executing action, the container entrypoint
   310	    becomes ``/bin/sh -c '<copy> && <checksum check> && exec "$@"'``:
   311	    the mounted snapshot is FIRST copied to PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH (the
   312	    container's own writable layer — never inside the mounted volume, so
   313	    no host-side process can touch it), the COPY's sha256 is verified,
   314	    and only then does freqtrade exec — reading the copy. This closes
   315	    the check-to-use race: the file freqtrade opens is never a path a
   316	    workspace-writer can replace, because check and use are the same
   317	    inode in container-private storage.
   318	
   319	    By default the container runs with the image's own lifecycle (root →
   320	    entrypoint chown of user_data to its internal ftuser → drop to
   321	    ftuser): freqtrade is pip-installed under ftuser's 0700 home, so an
   322	    explicit ``--user`` cannot reach the binary. Pass ``uid_gid``
   323	    explicitly only with an image that supports it."""
   324	    if image_ref != DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE or not DIGEST_RE.match(image_ref):
   325	        raise ValueError(
   326	            "image_ref must be exactly the pinned official reference "
   327	            f"{DEFAULT_PINNED_IMAGE!r}: got {image_ref!r}"
   328	        )
   329	    overlay = validate_extra_args(extra_args)
   330	    if overlay:
   331	        raise ValueError("; ".join(overlay))
   332	    data_findings = validate_data_args(data_args)
   333	    if data_findings:
   334	        raise ValueError("; ".join(data_findings))
   335	    if strategy is not None and not STRATEGY_RE.match(strategy):
   336	        # strategy is config-controlled text; it reaches docker argv and
   337	        # (in the hash-bound form) a shell script — only identifiers pass
   338	        raise ValueError(f"strategy {strategy!r} is not a plain identifier")
   339	    if not CONFIG_NAME_RE.match(config_name):
   340	        raise ValueError(f"config_name {config_name!r} has unsafe characters")
   341	
   342	    volume = f"{user_data_dir.resolve()}:/freqtrade/user_data"
   343	    config_arg = f"/freqtrade/user_data/{config_name}"
   344	    # In the sha-bound form freqtrade must read the CONTAINER-PRIVATE
   345	    # copy, never the mounted path (see PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH). The
   346	    # builder refuses a private path that would fall inside the mount.
   347	    if config_sha256 is not None and action in ("download-data", "dry-run"):
   348	        if PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH.startswith("/freqtrade/user_data/"):
   349	            raise ValueError(
   350	                "PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH must live outside the mounted volume"
   351	            )
   352	        exec_config_arg = PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH
   353	    else:
   354	        exec_config_arg = config_arg
   355	    cmd_args: List[str]
   356	    if action == "version":
   357	        cmd_args = ["--version"]
   358	    elif action == "download-data":
   359	        cmd_args = [
   360	            "download-data", "--config", exec_config_arg,
   361	            "--userdir", "/freqtrade/user_data",
   362	        ] + list(data_args or []) + list(extra_args or [])
   363	    elif action == "dry-run":
   364	        cmd_args = [
   365	            "trade", "--config", exec_config_arg,
   366	            "--userdir", "/freqtrade/user_data",
   367	            "--dry-run", "--strategy", strategy or "",
   368	        ] + list(extra_args or [])
   369	    else:
   370	        raise ValueError(f"unknown action {action!r}")
   371	
   372	    # Foreground commands self-clean with --rm; a detached smoke run must
   373	    # NOT use --rm, so a crash leaves the container's logs inspectable
   374	    # until we remove it explicitly after capturing evidence.
   375	    argv = ["docker", "run"]
   376	    if not detach:
   377	        argv.append("--rm")
   378	    argv += ["--name", name, "--stop-timeout", "60", "-v", volume]
   379	    if uid_gid:
   380	        argv += ["--user", uid_gid]
   381	    if detach:
   382	        argv.append("-d")
   383	
   384	    if config_sha256 is not None:
   385	        # Bind the config bytes INSIDE the container, at open time: the
   386	        # entrypoint COPIES the mounted snapshot to PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH
   387	        # (container-private storage — a host-side workspace writer
   388	        # cannot replace what it cannot address), verifies the COPY's
   389	        # sha256, and only then execs freqtrade, which reads the copy.
   390	        # The checksum and freqtrade's open() therefore act on the same
   391	        # unreachable-from-the-host inode: there is no window between
   392	        # check and use. Freqtrade args travel as positional parameters
   393	        # ("$@"), never through the shell string, so no token of them
   394	        # can inject.
   395	        if action not in ("download-data", "dry-run"):
   396	            raise ValueError(
   397	                "config_sha256 binding applies only to config-executing "
   398	                "actions"
   399	            )
   400	        if not SHA256_RE.match(config_sha256):
   401	            raise ValueError("config_sha256 must be a full sha256 hex digest")
   402	        script = (
   403	            f"cp {config_arg} {PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH} && "
   404	            f"printf '%s  %s\\n' {config_sha256} {PRIVATE_CONFIG_PATH} "
   405	            '| sha256sum -c --status - && exec "$@"'
   406	        )
   407	        # "$@" expands to the params AFTER argv0, so the full command —
   408	        # the freqtrade binary AND its subcommand — must be positional
   409	        # params; argv0 itself is never executed
   410	        argv += ["--entrypoint", "/bin/sh", image_ref, "-c", script,

  1024	def test_nofollow_hasher_refuses_symlinks(tmp_path):
  1025	    # round-14 Sol finding 2: the artifact hash goes through ONE
  1026	    # os.open with O_NOFOLLOW, so a final component swapped to a symlink
  1027	    # between the confinement check and the read is refused at open
  1028	    # (ELOOP) — the pointed-at bytes are never read, even when the
  1029	    # target is itself a regular file
  1030	    real = tmp_path / "real.txt"
  1031	    real.write_text("payload\n")
  1032	    link = tmp_path / "link.txt"
  1033	    link.symlink_to(real)
  1034	    try:
  1035	        fg.sha256_file_nofollow(link)
  1036	        raised = None
  1037	    except OSError as exc:
  1038	        raised = exc
  1039	    assert raised is not None
  1040	    assert raised.errno == errno.ELOOP
  1041	    # regular files still hash identically to the plain hasher
  1042	    assert fg.sha256_file_nofollow(real) == fg.sha256_file(real)

  1045	def test_nofollow_hasher_refuses_directories(tmp_path):
  1046	    # the OPENED descriptor must be a regular file (fstat), so a path
  1047	    # swapped to a directory mid-check cannot be walked by the reader
  1048	    d = tmp_path / "adir"
  1049	    d.mkdir()
  1050	    try:
  1051	        fg.sha256_file_nofollow(d)
  1052	        raised = None
  1053	    except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
  1054	        raised = exc
  1055	    assert raised is not None

  1058	def test_confined_hasher_refuses_intermediate_symlink_swap(tmp_path):
  1059	    # round-15 Sol finding 1: O_NOFOLLOW only guards the FINAL
  1060	    # component — a concurrent writer who swaps an INTERMEDIATE
  1061	    # directory for a symlink after the confinement check would be
  1062	    # followed by a plain open. The confined walker opens every
  1063	    # component relative to its verified parent fd with O_NOFOLLOW, so
  1064	    # the swapped state is refused with ELOOP at that component's open.
  1065	    # (Simulates the post-swap state directly: rel_parts were computed
  1066	    # while "sub" was still a real directory; then sub -> outside.)
  1067	    outside = tmp_path.parent / ("swap-outside-" + tmp_path.name)
  1068	    outside.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  1069	    (outside / "inner.json").write_text("outside bytes\n")
  1070	    (tmp_path / "sub").mkdir()
  1071	    (tmp_path / "sub" / "inner.json").write_text("inside bytes\n")
  1072	    rel = ("sub", "inner.json")
  1073	    # pre-swap: the walker hashes the in-repo file, matching the plain
  1074	    # hasher
  1075	    assert fg.sha256_file_confined(tmp_path, rel) == fg.sha256_file(
  1076	        tmp_path / "sub" / "inner.json")
  1077	    # post-swap: sub becomes a symlink to the outside dir
  1078	    shutil.rmtree(tmp_path / "sub")
  1079	    (tmp_path / "sub").symlink_to(outside)
  1080	    try:
  1081	        fg.sha256_file_confined(tmp_path, rel)
  1082	        raised = None
  1083	    except OSError as exc:
  1084	        raised = exc
  1085	    assert raised is not None
  1086	    # directory components are opened with O_DIRECTORY too, so a
  1087	    # swapped-to-symlink component refuses with ELOOP or ENOTDIR
  1088	    # (kernel-dependent order); both mean the symlink was NOT followed
  1089	    assert raised.errno in (errno.ELOOP, errno.ENOTDIR)
  1090	    # the closed gap, pinned: the final-component-only nofollow opener
  1091	    # WOULD have followed the swapped intermediate dir and hashed the
  1092	    # outside file — the walker must not
  1093	    assert fg.sha256_file_nofollow(
  1094	        tmp_path / "sub" / "inner.json") == fg.sha256_file(
  1095	        outside / "inner.json")
  1096	
  1097	
  1098	def test_confined_hasher_deep_real_paths_hash_plain(tmp_path):
  1099	    # legitimate deep in-repo paths (no symlinks anywhere) hash
  1100	    # identically to the plain hasher, including a single-component
  1101	    # relative path (leaf opened directly under the root fd)
  1102	    deep = tmp_path / "a" / "b" / "c"
  1103	    deep.mkdir(parents=True)
  1104	    (deep / "leaf.json").write_text("deep payload\n")
  1105	    (tmp_path / "flat.json").write_text("flat payload\n")

  1107	        tmp_path, ("a", "b", "c", "leaf.json")) == fg.sha256_file(

  1110	        tmp_path, ("flat.json",)) == fg.sha256_file(tmp_path / "flat.json")

## Verdict: FAIL

**Finding 1 — Low — root-path ancestor can still be followed.** [finance_goals.py](/home/agent/projects/odysseus-finance-r12/src/finance_goals.py:140) opens the absolute repo-root path with `O_NOFOLLOW`, which protects only that path’s final component. If a concurrent writer replaces a parent such as `/home/agent/projects` with a symlink after the [confinement check](/home/agent/projects/odysseus-finance-r12/src/finance_goals.py:342), the root open can follow it into an outside tree and the walker can hash an outside regular file. A read-only probe confirmed the kernel behavior: `O_NOFOLLOW` refused `/proc/self` but opened `/proc/self/task` through the symlinked ancestor. The claim that *no* path component can be followed is therefore too broad.

**Round-15 finding: fixed for repo-relative components.** The new directory-FD walk refuses an intermediate symlink below an opened root; its focused tests cover the swap and ordinary deep paths. Finding 1 concerns acquisition of the root FD itself.

The evidence structure is sound; the runs do **not** need re-execution or new receipts solely because the later commit changed only the evaluator. Git objects at HEAD match all **5 MT** and **10 FT** newest-receipt artifacts. Their `50b2b360` run commit is an ancestor of HEAD, and no cited artifact changed after the receipts were committed. The separate round-16 suite record reports **8,475 passed / 46 skipped / 5 xfailed**; the three finance test files contain **155 tests**. The receipt-cited round-15 record reports **8,472 / 152**.
